# KIP Coach Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Retire the 15-coach pixel cast. The AI coach becomes the user's own KIP in their colour. The Coach tab becomes the
**KIP** tab, and its page is W7 · Pocket room card: KIP's room as a framed Skia diorama over the conversation. Every
coach surface, string and icon follows, and the app icon becomes KIP's face (A).

**Architecture:**
- **Decoupling first** (Tasks 1–2). The kept thinking text, the frame clock and the coach status move out of the
  character modules, so later tasks can delete the cast without breaking anything:
  - `lib/thinkingText.ts`, `lib/kipCopy.ts`, `lib/useFrameClock.ts` and `lib/useScreenFocused.ts`;
  - `lib/coachStatus/CoachStatusProvider.tsx`, plus offline detection (`isNetworkError`).
  - Until Task 14, a compatibility shim keeps the old `useCharacterOptional()` callers compiling. They draw Mochi.
- **Backend** (Tasks 3–4):
  - persona set v5, one `kip` persona that every old id resolves to;
  - a data migration that stores `'kip'` for every user;
  - the old-app routes keep their shapes, and the consent texts name KIP without a version bump.
- **The room** (Tasks 5–8):
  - KIP gains four poses (`type` with three frames, `think`, `point`, `shrug`);
  - a pure, slot-driven room engine `lib/room/*` (170 × 110 cells, starter loadout);
  - `useTodayScene`, extracted from Home, feeds the window's weather;
  - `components/room/RoomView.tsx` records the static layers once into Skia pictures and draws KIP and the loop frame
    over them.
- **The page** (Tasks 9–10). `CoachScreen` keeps its phase logic and gains:
  - the room card, the status line and the state table (`lib/kipStage.ts`);
  - the collapse-on-scroll header, "Shrink room" and Reduce Motion.
- **Knock-ons** (Tasks 11–13): Ask KIP bars, Home, the tab and hub, Conversations, memory, Settings, consent screens,
  recaps, badges and every other `<Character>`.
- **Deletion and guards** (Task 14): the cast, its state, routes and tests go; a grep guard and a copy guard pin it.
- **Icon** (Task 15): a pure grid-to-pixels module, exported by `scripts/build-icons.mjs`, plus `expo-splash-screen`.
- **Verification** (Task 16): full suites, the eval runs and a simulator check.

**Tech Stack:**
- Backend: Express 5, Prisma 6 / Postgres, Jest with supertest.
- Mobile: Expo 57 / React Native 0.86 with NativeWind, React Navigation 7, Reanimated 4 and
  `@shopify/react-native-skia` 2.6.2 (already installed). Tests use Jest 30 with React Native Testing Library.
- Icons: `pngjs` 7.0.0 (new devDependency) and `sucrase-node` (already in `node_modules`).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-kip-coach-design.md` (Spec A).
- Its sibling `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-kip-room-design.md` (Spec B) is **not** in this plan. Spec B plugs
  into the room engine API built in Tasks 6 and 8, so build that API exactly as written:
  - `RoomLoadout`, `KipWorn`;
  - `drawRoom({ loadout, weather, night, kip, screen })` returning `kipSeat`, with `kip` as `{ colour, pose, mood, worn }`;
  - the memo keys, `FOCUS_BOX` and `THUMB_BOX`.
- Visual sources:
  - `docs/design/kip-coach/project/`: `W7.dc.html`, `K4.dc.html`, `States.dc.html`, `Elsewhere.dc.html` and
    `SetsV2.dc.html`, with the 2-point room helpers in `lib.js`, `lib2.js` and `sets2.js`;
  - `docs/design/app-icon/project/`: `Main.dc.html`, `Variants.dc.html` and `Icon.dc.html`, whose `C.face`, `C.block`
    and `C.a_*` concepts are the icon geometry.

## Global Constraints

- **Branch:** `feature/kip-coach-spec`, in the worktree
  `/Users/tushar/Documents/PROJECTS/Biometrics/.claude/worktrees/social-tab`. The code is main `f8d4afc`; the spec
  commits sit on top (HEAD `c41b2e7` before this plan).
  - Never switch branches, never push to main, never force-push, never bare `git stash`.
- **Commits:** plain `git commit -m "…"` with **no `Co-Authored-By` trailer and no mention of Claude, Cursor or AI**
  (owner rule; it applies to every subagent). The PR body carries no attribution footer either.
- **Subagents** run on model **opus**.
- **Every task keeps both full suites green at its commit.** Run the touched tests while working. Before the commit,
  run the full mobile suite, both typechecks and, for a backend task, the full backend suite.
- **Mobile commands**, from `mobile/`:
  - `export PATH=/Users/tushar/.nvm/versions/node/v24.21.0/bin:$PATH`
  - Tests: `node node_modules/.bin/jest --forceExit <paths>`. With no paths it runs the full suite.
  - Typecheck: `node node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit --types jest,node --pretty false | grep -c "error TS"`.
    - The baseline, **measured on this branch on 2026-10-14, is 10**: `__tests__/api/client.test.tsx` (2),
      `FactorBar.test.tsx` (1), `ScoreRing.test.tsx` (1), `MetricDetailScreen.test.tsx` (5) and `App.tsx` (1).
    - No task may add an error. Deleting a file that holds baseline errors may lower the count; record the new number
      in the ledger.
- **Backend commands**, from `backend/`. Never run two backend test runs at once, never point tests at the dev
  `biometrics` DB, and never print the database URL.
  - Tests go only through `.superpowers/sdd/2026-10-14-kip-coach/backend-jest.sh <paths>` (Task 0 copies it). It uses
    `TEST_DATABASE_URL` from `~/dev/biometrics-run/backend/.env` and Redis db 1.
  - A suite that fails or SIGSEGVs under full-suite load is rerun alone before it is treated as real.
  - Typecheck: `node node_modules/.bin/tsc --noEmit -p .` must report **0** errors. A fresh worktree needs
    `DATABASE_URL=postgresql://x@localhost/x npx prisma generate` first: generate needs the variable but never
    connects. Without it you see 3 stale `kipColour` errors.
  - After a schema or migration change, run the same `prisma generate`.
- **Buttons:**
  - Every button is `<Button>` from `mobile/src/components/ui/button.tsx`, with `rounded-lg`. No pills.
  - A raw `Pressable` / `PressableScale` with `accessibilityRole="button"` or `"link"` needs an entry in
    `mobile/__tests__/conventions/buttons.test.ts`, with a count and a reason, **in the task that adds it**.
  - An entry whose site disappears is removed **in the task that removes it**: a stale entry fails the guard.
- **Type:** A4 tokens only (`text-score`, `text-number`, `text-display`, `text-heading`, `text-headline`,
  `text-body`, `text-caption`, `text-fine`, `text-page-title`, `text-label`). The pixel bubble and labels use
  `font-pixel text-label`. No inline `fontSize` / `fontFamily`, and no arbitrary `text-[..px]`.
  `__tests__/conventions/typography.test.ts` must stay green; its EXEMPT counts are exact, so a task that removes an
  exempted inline size updates the count.
- **Copy:**
  - Every new user-visible string lives in a copy module: `lib/kipCopy.ts` (new), or the existing `homeCopy.ts`,
    `recoveryCopy.ts`, `sleepCopy.ts`, `profileCopy.ts` and `recapCopy.ts`.
  - Spoken labels never contain "·" (U+00B7) or "−" (U+2212).
  - The product name is **KIP** in capitals everywhere. "Coach" survives only in route, type and file names
    (Task 14's guard).
- **Privacy:**
  - Never log health values, dates, names, message text, room loadouts or the KIP colour.
  - Nothing new reaches the model: the colour, the room and the pose never enter a prompt.
  - Prefilled questions stay number-free (`lib/coachPrompts.ts`).
- **Dates:** never `new Date('YYYY-MM-DD')`.
- **Frozen files:** `components/social/CampScene.tsx` and `CampBanner.tsx` are never touched.
- **Shared test fixtures** live in `mobile/jest-mocks/`, never under `__tests__` (jest would run them as empty
  suites).
- **Skia in Jest:** `@shopify/react-native-skia` is mocked by `mobile/jest-mocks/skia.js`. Task 8 extends that mock;
  no test may load the real module.

## Plan rulings (made while writing the plan)

1. **A compatibility shim, not a big bang (Task 2).**
   - `CharacterProvider` goes in Task 2; `CoachStatusProvider` replaces it in `App.tsx`.
   - `characters/CharacterContext.ts` keeps its exports until Task 14. With no `CharacterContext.Provider` above it,
     `useCharacterOptional()` returns a value built from `CoachStatusProvider`: character `mochi`, `personaChosen:
     true`, no recovery band, the real status and thinking text, and no-op character choices.
   - So the not-yet-swapped screens keep compiling and drawing between Tasks 2 and 13, as Mochi. After Task 4 every
     stored persona is `kip`, which the old registry draws as Mochi anyway.
   - `withCharacter()` in tests still provides a real `CharacterContext`, so old tests keep passing until their
     surface is swapped.
2. **The starter room is authored new on the 170 × 110 grid.** The boards have no v2 starter set: `k4room.js` is K4's
   v1 130 × 80 layout, and `sets2.js` has only Spec B's six sets.
   - Task 6 ports K4's furnishing with the v2 helpers (`shell`, `gradV`, `poly`, `E`, `glow`, `shade`, `ao`, `sky2`,
     `daylight`, `nightVeil`).
   - The layout puts the desk and monitor on **KIP's left**. "Facing the monitor" is then the mirrored sprite, whose
     `glance` eyes look toward the desk, as spec §4 says literally.
   - Facing you, `point`'s arm (left side, columns 1–3) points at the monitor's chart.
3. **The `desk` slot's item is the Monitor**, as the spec's starter table says. It draws the desk, the chair and the
   monitor; its `style: 'standard'` is what `kipSeat` reads. `desk_item` is the mug.
4. **What each layer holds:**
   - `drawRoom` returns `{ base, light, emissive, frames, kip, kipSeat, focus, thumb }`. `kip` is the separate KIP
     layer the spec asks for.
   - `RoomView` draws `base` → `kip` → `light` → `emissive` → `frames[i]`, so the night veil and the daylight fall on
     KIP too, as on the boards.
   - The static picture is recorded as two Skia pictures, back (`base`) and front (`light` + `emissive`). Both are
     memoised by `roomPictureKey({ loadout, weather, night, colour })`.
5. **The monitor's screen content (lines, dots, chart) lives in the loop frames**, not the static picture. A state
   change (idle → thinking → answering) never rebuilds the picture. `screen` is an input of the frames only.
6. **`drawRoom`'s `kip` takes two optional extras**, beyond the required `{ colour, pose, mood, worn }`:
   - `frame` (0–2, the `type` loop);
   - `facing` (`'desk' | 'you'`; the default is `'desk'` for `type` and `'you'` otherwise).
   Spec B passes neither.
7. **The turn (spec §4)** plays two frames of 80 ms each, then settles:
   - `rest`/`glance` facing the desk;
   - `rest`/`glance` facing you;
   - then `point`/`glance`.
   Under Reduce Motion it cuts straight to `point`.
8. **Offline (spec §3.4):**
   - `useCoachConversation` maps a network error that arrives before any stream event to a new
     `CoachTurnError` kind, `'offline'`. Its user message is removed from the thread (not marked "not sent"), and its
     text goes back into the prompt bar if the bar is empty.
   - `useCoachStatus` / `CoachStatusProvider` hold `offline`, which the page reads.
   - A non-network status failure keeps today's "status unverified" behaviour: the chat stays usable and the line is
     renamed. The room shows it as `rest`/`sleepy`.
9. **Recap copy.** "{coach}'s note for the week" (spec §7.3) does not exist in the code. The strings that name the
   coach in the weekly story are "How {name} saw my week", "{name} says" and "Notes from {name}"
   (`WeeklyStoryView.tsx:212, 252, 310, 328`). They become "How KIP saw my week", "KIP says" and "Notes from KIP".
10. **Settings lives in Profile sections.** The spec's "Settings" group is drawn by `ProfileSectionScreen` sections
    `coach` (`YourCoachRow`) and `aiCoach` (`CoachSettingsSection`), with hub cards from `profileHub.ts` /
    `profileCopy.ts`. Task 12 merges them into one section, `kip`, titled "KIP", with one hub card.
11. **The colour helpers move before deletion.** `hexAlpha`, `contrast`, `mixHex`, `darkenHex` and `chipTextColor`
    (`components/characters/palette.ts` and `sprites/compose.ts`) move to `lib/colour.ts` in Task 1. Recaps, badges,
    the thinking row and the milestone tests import them from there.
12. **Backend persona ids.**
    - `findPersona` maps every id any registered set ever had (v1–v4, plus the `LEGACY_PERSONA_IDS` keys) to `kip`.
    - `RETIRED_CHARACTER_IDS`, `isRetiredCharacterId` and `canonicalPersonaId` are deleted, along with the
      persona PUT's retired fallback; every known id is simply `kip` now.
    - The v1–v4 files are not edited.
13. **The icon master** (1024 px from a 24-cell grid) is sampled nearest-neighbour: cell `floor(px × 24 / 1024)`. The
    Android files (36 cells → 432 px, 12 px a cell) and the favicon (24 → 48, 2 px a cell) are exact.
    - The tinted variant is J's block and eyes, in greyscale on transparent.
    - The splash is the engine sprite (rest/idle, coral) at 8 px a cell, centred.
14. **The dev gallery flag** becomes `EXPO_PUBLIC_KIP_GALLERY=1`, which renders `KipGalleryScreen`.
15. **The owner gate** is the simulator check (Task 16). No PR is opened by this plan; the controller reports and
    waits.

## Review Focus

1. **An old app talks to the new server.** An app from before this change does all of the following:
   `PUT /me/coach/persona {personaId:'luna'}`, then `{personaId:'encouraging'}`, then `GET /me/coach/status`.
   - Expected: 200 `{personaId:'kip'}` both times; status `personaChosen: true` and `personas` of length 1; the
     stored thinking attachment still comes back. Nothing returns 400 except junk ids.
   - Pinned in Task 4 (route tests for a v1 id, a v4 id and junk).
2. **A send fails while offline.** The user types a question in airplane mode and taps send.
   - Expected: the room shows KIP `rest`/`sleepy` with "Z Z"; the thread shows "KIP can't reach the internet. Ask
     again when you're back online." with no button; the question is back in the prompt bar, not in the thread.
   - Stop mid-answer, an HTTP 503 and a dropped stream are **not** offline.
   - Pinned in Task 2 (`isNetworkError`, `useCoachConversation` offline kind) and Task 9 (the page).
3. **A narrow phone and large text.** At a 320-point phone (280-point frame) and at the largest text size:
   - Expected: the diorama crops to exactly the focus box (window, desk and KIP all visible); the caption row's hot
     tag wraps under the caption rather than overflowing; the collapsed header's status line truncates to one line.
   - Pinned in Task 8 (`roomCrop` at 280, 323, 335 and 375) and Task 9 (the status line is one line). The caption
    row's wrap is a `flex-wrap` row, checked by eye at the largest text size in Task 16.
4. **The tab is left mid-answer, or Reduce Motion is switched on mid-turn.**
   - Expected: the loops and the typing stop the moment the tab blurs (the frame index freezes) and resume on focus;
     with Reduce Motion on, the turn cuts to `point` and the collapsed header switches with no fade.
   - Pinned in Task 8 (frame frozen when unfocused) and Task 10 (Reduce Motion cut and no fade).
5. **SecureStore throws (unsigned build, locked keychain) on first launch after the update.**
   - Expected: the `characterId` purge fails silently; "Shrink room" reads as expanded and a toggle still works for
     the session; the cached thinking text falls back to Steps.
   - Pinned in Task 2 (the purge swallows errors; the cache read falls back) and Task 10 (a shrink read failure
     leaves the card expanded).

- it draws KIP's head (not the coach) and says "KIP is typing it up";
- it keeps five text styles;
- the placeholder style no longer wraps the streaming answer.

Create `mobile/src/lib/thinkingText.ts`:

```ts
// "While KIP thinks" (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §1, Decoupling 1): how the chat shows KIP working on a reply.
// The app keeps five styles. The server still accepts all ten (backend/src/coach/thinking.ts) for older apps, so a
// stored tag, nameplate, bouncy, dialog or strip reads here as steps.
export const THINKING_TEXTS = ['lines', 'placeholder', 'typewriter', 'steps', 'shimmer'] as const;
export type ThinkingTextId = (typeof THINKING_TEXTS)[number];
export const DEFAULT_THINKING_TEXT: ThinkingTextId = 'steps';

export const isThinkingText = (v: unknown): v is ThinkingTextId =>
  typeof v === 'string' && (THINKING_TEXTS as readonly string[]).includes(v);

export function resolveThinkingText(v: unknown): ThinkingTextId {
  return isThinkingText(v) ? v : DEFAULT_THINKING_TEXT;
}

export const THINKING_TEXT_NAMES: Record<ThinkingTextId, { name: string; blurb: string }> = {
  lines: { name: 'KIP\'s lines', blurb: 'KIP says what it is up to, a new line every few seconds.' },
  placeholder: { name: 'Reply placeholder', blurb: 'A pulsing reply bubble where the answer will appear.' },
  typewriter: { name: 'Typewriter', blurb: 'KIP types its line letter by letter.' },
  steps: { name: 'Steps', blurb: 'The real steps, ticked off as they happen.' },
  shimmer: { name: 'Colour shimmer', blurb: 'A wash of KIP\'s colour, with a timer.' },
};
```

```ts
import { KIP_COLOURS, type KipColour } from './kip/kipArt';

const LABEL_CONTRAST = 4.5;

/**
 * KIP's colour for a "KIP" label on a card (spec §3.4), or null to use `foreground`: the colour only where it reads
 * at 4.5:1 on the dark card (all but ink and slate); light mode always uses foreground.
 */
export function kipLabelColour(colour: KipColour, scheme: 'light' | 'dark' | null | undefined): string | null {
  if (scheme !== 'dark') return null;
  const hex = KIP_COLOURS[colour];
  return contrast(hex, CARD.dark) >= LABEL_CONTRAST ? hex : null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `KipHead`**

Create `mobile/src/components/kip/KipHead.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useMemo } from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import Svg, { Rect } from 'react-native-svg';
import { KIP_GRID, kipArt, type KipColour, type KipMood, type KipPose } from '../../lib/kip/kipArt';
import { useMyKipColour } from '../../lib/kip/myKipColour';
import type { PixelRect } from '../../lib/weatherArt';

const HEAD_ROWS = 10;

/** Rows 0–9 of KIP's grid: the top of the block with its eyes (spec §7.2). */
export function kipHeadRects(v: { colour?: KipColour; mood?: KipMood; pose?: KipPose }): PixelRect[] {
  return kipArt(v).filter((r) => r.y < HEAD_ROWS);
}

// KIP's head: the Ask KIP bars (scale 4, 64 × 40) and the small icons on Home, the Conversations sheet, the memory
// screen, the thinking row and beside replies (scale 2, 32 × 20). Decorative. Defaults to your colour.
export function KipHead({ colour, mood = 'idle', scale = 2, testID }: {
  colour?: KipColour; mood?: KipMood; scale?: number; testID?: string;
}) {
  const mine = useMyKipColour();
  const s = Math.max(1, Math.floor(scale));
  const rects = useMemo(() => kipHeadRects({ colour: colour ?? mine, mood }), [colour, mine, mood]);
  return (
    <View
      testID={testID}
      accessible={false}
      accessibilityElementsHidden
      importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants"
      style={{ width: KIP_GRID * s, height: HEAD_ROWS * s }}
    >
      <Svg width={KIP_GRID * s} height={HEAD_ROWS * s} viewBox={`0 0 ${KIP_GRID} ${HEAD_ROWS}`}>
        {rects.map((p, i) => (
          <Rect key={i} x={p.x} y={p.y} width={p.w} height={p.h} fill={p.fill} />
        ))}
      </Svg>
    </View>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { useRef } from 'react';
import { useColorScheme } from 'nativewind';
import { chipTextColor } from '../../../lib/colour';
import { KIP_COLOURS } from '../../../lib/kip/kipArt';
import { useMyKipColour } from '../../../lib/kip/myKipColour';
import { KIP_NAME } from '../../../lib/kipCopy';
import { useFrameClock } from '../../../lib/useFrameClock';

/** KIP's name and colours for a thinking style: `accent` for fills, `text` reading at 4.6:1 on the row. */
export function useKipVoice() {
  const { colorScheme } = useColorScheme();
  const accent = KIP_COLOURS[useMyKipColour()];
  return { name: KIP_NAME, accent, text: chipTextColor(accent, colorScheme) };
}

/** Milliseconds since this style appeared, on the shared frame tick. Paused → 0: the first frame. */
export function useElapsed(paused: boolean): number {
  const t = useFrameClock(paused);
  const mountedAt = useRef(Date.now());
  return paused || t === 0 ? 0 : Math.max(0, Date.now() - mountedAt.current);
}
```

`useThinkingLine.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { KIP_THINKING_VERBS } from '../../../lib/kipCopy';

export const LINE_MS = 2400;

// KIP's line for the thinking text (spec §1 Decoupling 2), moving to the next of three every 2.4 s. Paused holds it.
export function useThinkingLine(paused: boolean): { line: string; index: number } {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (paused) return undefined;
    const timer = setInterval(() => setIndex((i) => (i + 1) % KIP_THINKING_VERBS.length), LINE_MS);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [paused]);
  return { line: KIP_THINKING_VERBS[index]!, index };
}
```

`ThinkingRow.tsx` becomes:

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import { useReducedMotion } from 'react-native-reanimated';
import { KipHead } from '../../kip/KipHead';
import type { ThinkingTextId } from '../../../lib/thinkingText';
import { KIP_COPY } from '../../../lib/kipCopy';
import { Lines } from './Lines';
import { Placeholder } from './Placeholder';
import { spoken } from './shared';
import { Shimmer } from './Shimmer';
import { Steps } from './Steps';
import type { ThinkingStyleProps } from './types';
import { Typewriter } from './Typewriter';

export type { ThinkingStyleProps } from './types';

const STYLES: Record<ThinkingTextId, React.ComponentType<ThinkingStyleProps>> = {
  lines: Lines,
  placeholder: Placeholder,
  typewriter: Typewriter,
  steps: Steps,
  shimmer: Shimmer,
};

/** Steps speaks the step it is on; every other style a steady "KIP is typing it up" (no re-announce per line). */
export function thinkingRowLabel(style: ThinkingTextId, props: ThinkingStyleProps): string {
  if (style === 'steps' && props.steps.length) {
    const current = props.steps.find((step) => !step.done) ?? props.steps[props.steps.length - 1]!;
    return spoken(current.label);
  }
  return KIP_COPY.thinkingLabel;
}

// The KIP page's "a reply is on its way" row (spec §3.4 Thinking): KIP's head, glancing, and the chosen thinking
// text. Reduce Motion freezes every style on its first frame.
export function ThinkingRow({ style, steps, paused, testID }: ThinkingStyleProps & { style: ThinkingTextId; testID?: string }) {
  const reduceMotion = useReducedMotion();
  const props: ThinkingStyleProps = { steps, paused: paused || reduceMotion };
  const Body = STYLES[style];
  return (
    <View
      testID={testID}
      accessible
      accessibilityRole="text"
      accessibilityLabel={thinkingRowLabel(style, props)}
      accessibilityLiveRegion="polite"
      className="flex-row items-end gap-1.5"
    >
      <View className="pb-3">
        <KipHead testID="thinking-kip-head" mood="glance" />
      </View>
      <View className="flex-1">
        <Body {...props} />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `isNetworkError`**

In `mobile/src/api/client.ts`, add above `apiResponse`:

```ts
// Offline detection without NetInfo (spec §3.4, owner 2026-10-14): fetch itself rejected, before any response.
// Tagged where it happens so nothing downstream (an AbortError from Stop, a parse error, an ApiError) can pass for it.
const NETWORK = Symbol.for('biometrics.network');

export function markNetworkError<E>(err: E): E {
  if (err && typeof err === 'object') (err as Record<symbol, unknown>)[NETWORK] = true;
  return err;
}

export function isNetworkError(err: unknown): boolean {
  if (!err || typeof err !== 'object' || (err as { name?: unknown }).name === 'AbortError') return false;
  return (err as Record<symbol, unknown>)[NETWORK] === true;
}
```

Inside `apiResponse`, wrap the `fetchImpl(...)` call:

```ts
  let res: Response;
  try {
    res = await fetchImpl(`${baseUrl}${path}`, { ...options, credentials: 'omit', headers: { ...options.headers, Cookie: cookie } });
  } catch (err) {
    // An abort is the caller's own Stop, never "offline".
    throw (err as { name?: unknown })?.name === 'AbortError' ? err : markNetworkError(err);
  }
```

Keep the existing arguments exactly as they are today; only the `try` is new. `streamCoachMessage` goes through
`apiResponse` (`coachStream.ts:132`), so streams are covered too. `mapCoachError` in `api/coach.ts:336` must pass a
tagged error through unchanged. If it wraps unknown errors, add a first line:
`if (isNetworkError(error)) return error as Error;`.

- [ ] **Step 4: The cache and the provider**

Create `mobile/src/lib/coachStatus/thinkingCache.ts`:

```ts
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { resolveThinkingText, type ThinkingTextId } from '../thinkingText';

// The device cache (spec §1 Decoupling 5): `thinking` holds the last thinking text this device showed, so a cold
// start draws it before the status arrives. It is a look only, so every keychain failure is swallowed (as
// theme/preference.ts does). The retired `characterId` key is deleted once.
const THINKING_KEY = 'thinking';
const RETIRED_CHARACTER_KEY = 'characterId';

export async function readCachedThinkingText(): Promise<ThinkingTextId | null> {
  try {
    const raw = await SecureStore.getItemAsync(THINKING_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    const v: unknown = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!v || typeof v !== 'object' || Array.isArray(v)) return null;
    return resolveThinkingText((v as { text?: unknown }).text);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export async function writeCachedThinkingText(text: ThinkingTextId): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(THINKING_KEY, JSON.stringify({ text }));
  } catch {
    // The next cold start shows Steps until the status arrives.
  }
}

export async function clearCachedThinking(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(THINKING_KEY);
  } catch {
    // Replaced by the next account's status.
  }
}

export async function purgeCachedCharacter(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await SecureStore.deleteItemAsync(RETIRED_CHARACTER_KEY);
  } catch {
    // Nothing reads it any more.
  }
}
```

Create `mobile/src/lib/coachStatus/CoachStatusProvider.tsx`. Port `CharacterProvider.tsx`'s account and epoch logic
(`accountKey`, the `epoch` / `signedIn` refs, the `[account]` effect), keeping only what is listed below:

```tsx
import React, { createContext, useCallback, useContext, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { fetchCoachStatus, setCoachThinking, type CoachStatusDTO } from '../../api/coach';
import { isNetworkError } from '../../api/client';
import { useOptionalAuth } from '../../auth/AuthContext';
import { DEFAULT_THINKING_TEXT, isThinkingText, resolveThinkingText, type ThinkingTextId } from '../thinkingText';
import { clearCachedThinking, purgeCachedCharacter, readCachedThinkingText, writeCachedThinkingText } from './thinkingCache';

export interface CoachStatusValue {
  /** null = not known (yet) or the read failed with a server error; treated like a disabled coach. */
  status: CoachStatusDTO | null;
  statusLoaded: boolean;
  /** The last status read or send failed before reaching the server (spec §3.4); cleared by the next success. */
  offline: boolean;
  thinkingText: ThinkingTextId;
  refreshStatus(): Promise<void>;
  setStatus(next: CoachStatusDTO | null): void;
  setOffline(next: boolean): void;
  /** Optimistic: switches at once, reverts and rethrows if the save fails. */
  chooseThinkingText(text: ThinkingTextId): Promise<void>;
}

const CoachStatusContext = createContext<CoachStatusValue | null>(null);

export function useCoachStatusContext(): CoachStatusValue | null {
  return useContext(CoachStatusContext);
}

let purged = false;

function accountKey(userId: string | null, isPending: boolean): string {
  if (userId !== null) return `user:${userId}`;
  return isPending ? 'pending' : 'signed-out';
}

// The coach status for the whole app (spec §1 Decoupling 4): fetched once per account, shared by every
// useCoachStatus() caller, and nothing about a character.
export function CoachStatusProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }): React.ReactElement {
  const auth = useOptionalAuth();
  const account = accountKey(auth?.session?.userId ?? null, auth?.isPending ?? false);
  const [status, setStatusState] = useState<CoachStatusDTO | null>(null);
  const [statusLoaded, setStatusLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [offline, setOffline] = useState(false);
  const [thinkingText, setThinkingText] = useState<ThinkingTextId>(DEFAULT_THINKING_TEXT);
  const epoch = useRef(0);
  const signedIn = useRef(false);
  const thinkingKnown = useRef(false);
  const pendingThinking = useRef(0);
  const thinkingRef = useRef<ThinkingTextId>(DEFAULT_THINKING_TEXT);

  const showThinking = useCallback((next: ThinkingTextId) => {
    thinkingRef.current = next;
    setThinkingText(next);
  }, []);

  const setStatus = useCallback(
    (next: CoachStatusDTO | null) => {
      setStatusState(next);
      if (next && pendingThinking.current === 0) {
        const text = resolveThinkingText(next.thinkingText);
        thinkingKnown.current = true;
        showThinking(text);
        void writeCachedThinkingText(text);
      }
    },
    [showThinking],
  );

  const refreshStatus = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!signedIn.current) return;
    const started = epoch.current;
    try {
      const next = await fetchCoachStatus();
      if (epoch.current !== started) return;
      setOffline(false);
      setStatus(next);
    } catch (err) {
      if (epoch.current !== started) return;
      // Offline keeps the last known status; any other failure is unknown, as before.
      if (isNetworkError(err)) setOffline(true);
      else setStatusState(null);
    }
    setStatusLoaded(true);
  }, [setStatus]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (purged) return;
    purged = true;
    void purgeCachedCharacter();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    epoch.current += 1;
    const started = epoch.current;
    signedIn.current = account.startsWith('user:');
    thinkingKnown.current = false;
    pendingThinking.current = 0;
    setStatusState(null);
    setStatusLoaded(false);
    setOffline(false);
    if (account === 'signed-out') {
      showThinking(DEFAULT_THINKING_TEXT);
      void clearCachedThinking();
      return;
    }
    void readCachedThinkingText().then((cached) => {
      if (cached && epoch.current === started && !thinkingKnown.current) showThinking(cached);
    });
    if (signedIn.current) void refreshStatus();
  }, [account, refreshStatus, showThinking]);

  useEffect(() => () => { epoch.current += 1; }, []);

  const chooseThinkingText = useCallback(
    async (text: ThinkingTextId) => {
      if (!signedIn.current) return;
      if (!isThinkingText(text)) throw new Error(`Unknown thinking text: ${String(text)}`);
      const started = epoch.current;
      const previous = thinkingRef.current;
      pendingThinking.current += 1;
      thinkingKnown.current = true;
      showThinking(text);
      setStatusState((s) => (s ? { ...s, thinkingText: text } : s));
      void writeCachedThinkingText(text);
      try {
        await setCoachThinking({ text });
      } catch (error) {
        if (epoch.current === started && thinkingRef.current === text) {
          showThinking(previous);
          setStatusState((s) => (s ? { ...s, thinkingText: previous } : s));
          void writeCachedThinkingText(previous);
        }
        throw error;
      } finally {
        if (epoch.current === started) pendingThinking.current -= 1;
      }
    },
    [showThinking],
  );

  const value = useMemo<CoachStatusValue>(
    () => ({ status, statusLoaded, offline, thinkingText, refreshStatus, setStatus, setOffline, chooseThinkingText }),
    [status, statusLoaded, offline, thinkingText, refreshStatus, setStatus, chooseThinkingText],
  );
  return <CoachStatusContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</CoachStatusContext.Provider>;
}
```

In `mobile/src/characters/CharacterContext.ts`, keep the interface and the `CharacterContext` object, and replace the two hooks:

```ts
import { useCoachStatusContext } from '../lib/coachStatus/CoachStatusProvider';
import { DEFAULT_THINKING_ATTACHMENT } from '../components/characters/thinking';

const noChoice = async () => {};

// Ruling 1 (plan 2026-10-14-kip-coach): until Task 14 deletes it, an old caller with no CharacterContext.Provider
// above it gets the coach status from CoachStatusProvider and Mochi as the look.
export function useCharacterOptional(): CharacterContextValue | null {
  const explicit = useContext(CharacterContext);
  const coach = useCoachStatusContext();
  if (explicit) return explicit;
  if (!coach) return null;
  return {
    characterId: 'mochi',
    personaChosen: true,
    status: coach.status,
    statusLoaded: coach.statusLoaded,
    recoveryBand: null,
    thinkingAttachment: DEFAULT_THINKING_ATTACHMENT,
    thinkingText: coach.thinkingText,
    refreshStatus: coach.refreshStatus,
    chooseCharacter: noChoice,
    chooseThinking: async ({ text }) => { if (text) await coach.chooseThinkingText(text); },
  };
}

export function useCharacter(): CharacterContextValue {
  const value = useCharacterOptional();
  if (!value) throw new Error('useCharacter must be used inside CoachStatusProvider');
  return value;
}
```

Both hooks always call `useContext` twice, so the hook order is stable. `thinkingText` keeps its `ThinkingTextId` type,
which is now `lib/thinkingText`'s (Task 1's re-export).

Rewrite `mobile/src/lib/useCoachStatus.ts`. Keep `coachEntryRoute` and `CoachEntryRoute`, and add local `offline` for the
no-provider fallback:

```ts
export function useCoachStatus(navigation?: FocusSource) {
  const shared = useCoachStatusContext();
  const [localStatus, setLocalStatus] = useState<CoachStatusDTO | null>(null);
  const [localOffline, setLocalOffline] = useState(false);
  const hasProvider = shared !== null;

  const localRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      setLocalStatus(await fetchCoachStatus());
      setLocalOffline(false);
    } catch (err) {
      if (isNetworkError(err)) setLocalOffline(true);
      else setLocalStatus(null);
    }
  }, []);
  const refresh = shared ? shared.refreshStatus : localRefresh;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (hasProvider) return;
    void localRefresh();
  }, [hasProvider, localRefresh]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const unsubscribe = navigation?.addListener?.('focus', () => {
      void refresh();
    });
    return unsubscribe;
  }, [navigation, refresh]);

  if (shared) return { status: shared.status, setStatus: shared.setStatus, refresh, offline: shared.offline, setOffline: shared.setOffline };
  return { status: localStatus, setStatus: setLocalStatus, refresh, offline: localOffline, setOffline: setLocalOffline };
}
```

The old effect guarded against setting state after unmount with a `cancelled` flag; keep that by adding a `mounted`
ref.

```ts
// Persona set v5 (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §2): the coach is the user's own KIP. One persona; every id any older
// set ever had resolves to it (index.ts). v1–v4 stay registered and unedited (see v1.ts's header).
import { REQUIRED_DISALLOWED_TOPICS, type CoachPersona, type PersonaSet } from './types';

export const KIP_PERSONA: CoachPersona = {
  id: 'kip',
  name: 'KIP',
  tone: "Friendly, plain-spoken and a little playful: the user's own small coach who knows their data. Calm about a dip, glad about a win, never preachy or gushing.",
  focus: 'Whatever the question is about: sleep, recovery, training or steady habits.',
  verbosity: 'normal',
  // As v4: the weekly note keeps running.
  proactivity: 'threshold-triggered',
  disallowedTopics: [...REQUIRED_DISALLOWED_TOPICS, 'supplement recommendations'],
};

export const v5Personas: PersonaSet = { version: 'v5', defaultPersonaId: 'kip', personas: [KIP_PERSONA] };
```

In `backend/src/coach/personas/index.ts`:
- Register `[v5Personas.version]: v5Personas` in `PERSONA_SETS`.
- Set `LIVE_PERSONA_VERSION = 'v5'`.

- Replace `findPersona` and `resolvePersona` with:

```ts
// Every id any registered set ever had, plus the v1 → v2 legacy keys: an old app's request never fails (§2).
const KNOWN_IDS: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
  ...Object.values(PERSONA_SETS).flatMap((set) => set.personas.map((p) => p.id)),
  ...Object.keys(LEGACY_PERSONA_IDS),
]);

export function isKnownPersonaId(id: unknown): boolean {
  return typeof id === 'string' && KNOWN_IDS.has(id);
}

/** The live persona for any id the app ever stored or sent; undefined for anything else. */
export function findPersona(id: unknown): CoachPersona | undefined {
  return isKnownPersonaId(id) ? liveSet().personas[0] : undefined;
}

export function resolvePersona(id: string | null | undefined): CoachPersona {
  return findPersona(id) ?? findPersona(DEFAULT_PERSONA_ID)!;
}
```

import { readFileSync } from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { prisma } from '../../src/db/client';
import { migrateTestDb } from '../setupTestDb';
import { createUser } from '../coach/helpers';

const NAME = '20261014120000_kip_coach';
const SQL = readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, `../../prisma/migrations/${NAME}/migration.sql`), 'utf8');
// Strip comments before splitting: the migration's comments contain semicolons.
const UPDATE = SQL.replace(/--.*$/gm, '').split(';').map((s) => s.trim()).find((s) => s.startsWith('UPDATE'))!;

beforeAll(() => migrateTestDb());
afterAll(() => prisma.$disconnect());

describe(NAME, () => {
  it('is data only: one UPDATE, nothing dropped or altered', () => {
    const statements = SQL.replace(/--.*$/gm, '').split(';').map((s) => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
    expect(statements).toEqual([UPDATE]);
    expect(SQL).not.toMatch(/\b(DROP|ALTER)\b/i);

  it('stores kip for every user and leaves the thinking text, digests and recaps alone', async () => {

    await prisma.$executeRawUnsafe(UPDATE);

    expect(rows).toHaveLength(4);

    expect((await prisma.coachDigest.findUniqueOrThrow({ where: { id: digest.id } })).personaId).toBe('mochi');
  });
});

Rewrite the persona PUT handler:

```ts
  // Older apps still send a character id; every known id saves KIP (spec §2). Dropped in the cleanup release.
  router.put('/me/coach/persona', async (req, res) => {
    const userId = req.userId!;
    const persona = findPersona(req.body?.personaId);
    if (!persona) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: 'unknown_persona' });
      return;
    }
    try {
      const result = await prisma.user.updateMany({ where: { id: userId }, data: { coachPersonaId: persona.id } });
      if (result.count === 0) {
        res.status(404).json({ error: 'User not found' });
        return;
      }
      res.json({ personaId: persona.id });
    } catch (err) {
      logFailure('persona_save', err);
      res.status(500).json({ error: 'coach_unavailable' });
    }
  });
```

Keep the router variable, the auth middleware and the log helper names the file already uses; only the body logic
changes. Remove the now-unused imports (`DEFAULT_PERSONA_ID`, and `clearTodaySummary` if nothing else in the file uses it).

```ts
describe('KIP Coach poses (spec 2026-10-14 §4)', () => {
  const NEW = ['type', 'think', 'point', 'shrug'] as const;
  const COLOURS = ['coral', 'cobalt', 'lime'] as const;

  it.each(NEW)('%s only adds arm cells in cells rest leaves empty (body, legs, eyes untouched)', (pose) => {
    for (const mood of KIP_MOODS) for (let frame = 0; frame < kipPoseFrames(pose); frame++) {
      const rest = kipGrid({ pose: 'rest', mood });
      const posed = kipGrid({ pose, mood, frame });
      posed.forEach((row, y) => row.forEach((c, x) => {
        const was = rest[y]![x];
        if (was !== null) expect(c).toBe(was);
        else if (c !== null) expect([kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS.coral).body, kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS.coral).shade]).toContain(c);
      }));
    }
  });

  it('type has three frames that differ only in which arm is down', () => {
    expect(kipPoseFrames('type')).toBe(3);
    for (const p of ['rest', 'think', 'point', 'shrug'] as const) expect(kipPoseFrames(p)).toBe(1);
    const frames = [0, 1, 2].map((frame) => ascii(kipGrid({ pose: 'type', frame }), 'coral'));
    expect(new Set(frames.map((f) => f.join('\n'))).size).toBe(3);
    expect(frames[0]![11]).toBe('..bbbbbbbbbsss..');
    expect(frames[1]![12]).toBe('..bbbbbbbbbs....');
    expect(frames[2]![12]).toBe('....bbbbbbbsss..');
  });

  it('a frame number wraps, so a free-running clock can pass any integer', () => {
    expect(kipGrid({ pose: 'type', frame: 4 })).toEqual(kipGrid({ pose: 'type', frame: 1 }));
    expect(kipGrid({ pose: 'point', frame: 2 })).toEqual(kipGrid({ pose: 'point' }));
  });

  it('point reaches out at shoulder height on the left; shrug lifts both arms', () => {
    expect(ascii(kipGrid({ pose: 'point' }), 'coral')[8]).toBe('.bbbbbebbebs....');
    const shrug = ascii(kipGrid({ pose: 'shrug' }), 'coral');
    expect(shrug[7]).toBe('..b.bbbbbbbs.s..');
    expect(shrug[8]).toBe('...bbbebbebss...');
  });

  it('mirrorGrid flips left and right and nothing else', () => {
    const g = kipGrid({ pose: 'point', mood: 'glance' });
    const m = mirrorGrid(g);
    g.forEach((row, y) => expect(m[y]).toEqual([...row].reverse()));
    expect(mirrorGrid(m)).toEqual(g);
  });

  it.each(NEW.flatMap((pose) => COLOURS.map((colour) => [pose, colour] as const)))('%s in %s matches its snapshot', (pose, colour) => {
    const frames = Array.from({ length: kipPoseFrames(pose) }, (_, frame) => ascii(kipGrid({ colour, pose, frame }), colour).join('\n'));
    expect(frames).toMatchSnapshot();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The poses**

In `mobile/src/lib/kip/kipArt.ts`:

```ts
export const KIP_POSES = ['rest', 'wave', 'cheer', 'bottle', 'coffee', 'stretch', 'type', 'think', 'point', 'shrug'] as const;
```

Add `frame?: number;` to `KipVariant`, with the doc comment `/** The loop frame, 0–2. Only `type` has three; every other pose ignores it. */`.

Replace the `PoseDef` type and extend `POSES`:

```ts
type Arm = readonly [number, number, Role];
type PoseDef = {
  arms?: readonly Arm[];
  /** Per-frame arms for a looping pose; overrides `arms`. */
  frames?: readonly (readonly Arm[])[];
  hop?: number; tall?: boolean; eyes?: EyeMode; held?: keyof typeof HELD;
};
const POSES: Record<KipPose, PoseDef> = {
  // … the six existing poses, unchanged …
  // KIP Coach (spec 2026-10-14 §4). Typing: both arms forward and low; each frame drops one arm a row.
  type: {
    frames: [
      [[3, 11, 'b'], [2, 11, 'b'], [12, 11, 's'], [13, 11, 's']],
      [[3, 12, 'b'], [2, 12, 'b'], [12, 11, 's'], [13, 11, 's']],
      [[3, 11, 'b'], [2, 11, 'b'], [12, 12, 's'], [13, 12, 's']],
    ],
  },
  // One arm up to the chin.
  think: { arms: [[12, 11, 's'], [13, 10, 's'], [13, 9, 's'], [12, 8, 's']] },
  // One arm out to the side at shoulder height.
  point: { arms: [[3, 8, 'b'], [2, 8, 'b'], [1, 8, 'b']] },
  // Both arms out and up.
  shrug: { arms: [[3, 8, 'b'], [2, 7, 'b'], [12, 8, 's'], [13, 7, 's']] },
};

/** How many loop frames a pose has (§4: `type` alternates its arms over three). */
export function kipPoseFrames(pose: KipPose): number {
  return POSES[pose].frames?.length ?? 1;
}

/** The sprite mirrored left–right: KIP facing the desk (§4). No back or side view exists. */
export function mirrorGrid(grid: KipGrid): KipGrid {
  return grid.map((row) => [...row].reverse());
}
```

- The signature becomes
  `export function kipGrid({ colour = DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR, pose = 'rest', mood = 'idle', frame = 0 }: KipVariant): KipGrid {`.

Create `mobile/src/screens/dev/KipGalleryScreen.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { ScrollView, View } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { Kip } from '../../components/kip/Kip';
import { Text } from '../../components/ui/text';
import { KIP_MOODS, KIP_POSES, kipPoseFrames, type KipColour } from '../../lib/kip/kipArt';

const COLOURS: KipColour[] = ['coral', 'cobalt', 'lime'];

// Dev only (EXPO_PUBLIC_KIP_GALLERY=1): every pose × mood × the States board's three colours (spec §4). Replaces the
// character gallery. The `type` pose shows frame 0; its loop is checked in the room.
export function KipGalleryScreen() {
  return (
    <SafeAreaView className="flex-1 bg-background">
      <ScrollView contentContainerStyle={{ padding: 16, gap: 20 }}>
        {COLOURS.map((colour) => (
          <View key={colour} className="gap-3">
            <Text className="text-heading">{colour}</Text>
            {KIP_POSES.map((pose) => (
              <View key={pose} className="gap-1">
                <Text className="text-caption text-muted-foreground">{`${pose}${kipPoseFrames(pose) > 1 ? ' (3 frames)' : ''}`}</Text>
                <View className="flex-row flex-wrap gap-2">
                  {KIP_MOODS.map((mood) => (
                    <Kip key={mood} testID={`kip-gallery-${colour}-${pose}-${mood}`} colour={colour} pose={pose} mood={mood} scale={3} />
                  ))}
                </View>
              </View>
            ))}
          </View>
        ))}
      </ScrollView>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}
```

### Task 6: The room engine, `lib/room/*` (pure, starter room only)

Spec §5 Modules and The starter furnishing, §6 window states. No React, no Skia. Plan rulings 2–6 apply.

The code below was run on 2026-10-14 against the Task 5 KIP engine with `sucrase-node`, and the boxes were checked:
- KIP at every pose, mood and frame sits inside both boxes;
- each starter item sits inside its slot anchor;
- the weather markers appear where they should;
- `drawRoom` took 0.45 ms in Node.

**Files:**
- Create: `mobile/src/lib/room/grid.ts`, `paint.ts`, `weather.ts`, `slots.ts`, `loadout.ts`, `items.ts`,
  `starterItems.ts`, `draw.ts`
- Test (create): `mobile/__tests__/lib/room.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `kipGrid`, `kipRamp`, `KIP_COLOURS`, `KIP_GRID`, `mirrorGrid`, `KipColour`, `KipMood`, `KipPose` (Task 5);
  - `mixHex`, `darkenHex` (Task 1, `lib/colour.ts`);
  - `SceneKey` (`lib/home/scene.ts`).
- Produces (Spec B depends on these names; do not rename):
  - `ROOM_W`, `ROOM_H`, `CELL_PT`, `FLOOR_Y`, `FOCUS_BOX`, `THUMB_BOX`, `type Box`, `type RoomRect`, `boundsOf`,
    `boxContains`
  - `ROOM_SLOTS`, `type RoomSlot`, `WORN_SLOTS`, `type WornSlot`, `SLOT_ANCHORS`, `DRAW_ORDER`
  - `type ItemId`, `type RoomLoadout`, `type KipWorn`, `STARTER_LOADOUT`, `EMPTY_WORN`, `loadoutKey`, `wornKey`
  - `type ItemArt`, `type ItemContext`, `type ItemLayers`, `type MonitorScreen`, `type SeatStyle`, `registerItems`,
    `itemArt`
  - `STARTER_ITEMS`, `MONITOR_GLASS`
  - `type RoomWeather`, `roomWeatherOf(key: SceneKey)`, `windowSky`, `SUN`, `RAIN`, `NIGHT_VEIL`
  - `type KipSeat`, `SEATS`, `kipSeatFor`, `type RoomKip`, `type RoomInput`, `type RoomDrawing`, `drawRoom`,
    `drawRoomStatic`, `drawRoomFrames`, `drawKipLayer`, `roomPictureKey`, `kipLayerKey`, `KIP_CELLS`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test**

Create `mobile/__tests__/lib/room.test.ts`:

```ts
import { drawKipLayer, drawRoom, drawRoomFrames, drawRoomStatic, kipLayerKey, kipSeatFor, roomPictureKey, SEATS, type RoomInput } from '../../src/lib/room/draw';
import { boundsOf, boxContains, FOCUS_BOX, ROOM_H, ROOM_W, THUMB_BOX, type RoomRect } from '../../src/lib/room/grid';
import { itemArt, type ItemContext } from '../../src/lib/room/items';
import { EMPTY_WORN, STARTER_LOADOUT, type RoomLoadout } from '../../src/lib/room/loadout';
import { ROOM_SLOTS, SLOT_ANCHORS, WORN_SLOTS } from '../../src/lib/room/slots';
import { MONITOR_GLASS, STARTER_ITEMS } from '../../src/lib/room/starterItems';
import { NIGHT_VEIL, RAIN, roomWeatherOf, SUN, type RoomWeather } from '../../src/lib/room/weather';
import { KIP_COLOURS, KIP_MOODS, KIP_POSES, kipRamp } from '../../src/lib/kip/kipArt';

const KIP = { colour: 'coral' as const, pose: 'rest' as const, mood: 'idle' as const, worn: EMPTY_WORN };
const INPUT: RoomInput = { loadout: STARTER_LOADOUT, weather: 'cloudy', night: false, kip: KIP, screen: 'lines' };
const CTX: ItemContext = { weather: 'cloudy', night: false, colour: 'coral', ramp: kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS.coral), screen: 'lines' };
const WEATHERS: RoomWeather[] = ['clear', 'mostly', 'cloudy', 'stormy', 'short', 'fallback'];
const has = (rects: readonly RoomRect[], colour: string) => rects.some((q) => q[4] === colour);
const staticOf = (loadout: RoomLoadout, weather: RoomWeather = 'cloudy', night = false) =>
  drawRoomStatic({ loadout, weather, night, colour: 'coral' });

describe('the grid and the slots', () => {
  it('is 170 × 110 cells, with the focus and thumbnail boxes the spec fixes', () => {
    expect([ROOM_W, ROOM_H]).toEqual([170, 110]);
    expect(FOCUS_BOX).toEqual({ x: 15, y: 10, w: 140, h: 100 });
    expect(THUMB_BOX).toEqual({ x: 70, y: 40, w: 96, h: 60 });
    const grid = { x: 0, y: 0, w: ROOM_W, h: ROOM_H };
    expect(boxContains(grid, FOCUS_BOX) && boxContains(grid, THUMB_BOX)).toBe(true);
  });

  it('has the 12 room slots and the 4 worn slots, every anchor inside the grid', () => {
    expect(ROOM_SLOTS).toEqual(['wallpaper', 'floor', 'art', 'window', 'shelf', 'desk', 'desk_item', 'plant', 'lamp', 'corner', 'rug', 'pet']);
    expect(WORN_SLOTS).toEqual(['head', 'face', 'neck', 'ears']);
    for (const slot of ROOM_SLOTS) expect(boxContains({ x: 0, y: 0, w: ROOM_W, h: ROOM_H }, SLOT_ANCHORS[slot])).toBe(true);
  });

  it('registers the ten placed starter items, each drawn inside its slot anchor', () => {
    expect(STARTER_ITEMS.map((i) => i.id).sort()).toEqual(
      ['amber_lamp', 'books', 'dark_oak', 'fern', 'monitor', 'mug', 'plain_frame', 'plain_slate', 'slate_rug', 'wall_calendar'],
    );
    for (const item of STARTER_ITEMS) {
      expect(STARTER_LOADOUT[item.slot]).toBe(item.id);
      expect({ id: item.id, inside: boxContains(SLOT_ANCHORS[item.slot], boundsOf(item.draw(CTX).base ?? [])!) }).toEqual({ id: item.id, inside: true });
    }
    expect([STARTER_LOADOUT.corner, STARTER_LOADOUT.pet]).toEqual([null, null]);
  });
});

describe('drawRoom', () => {
  it('is deterministic for the starter loadout', () => {
    expect(drawRoom(INPUT)).toEqual(drawRoom(INPUT));
  });

  it('an empty slot draws nothing: removing an item removes exactly its rects', () => {
    const full = staticOf(STARTER_LOADOUT).base.length;
    for (const item of STARTER_ITEMS) {
      const without = staticOf({ ...STARTER_LOADOUT, [item.slot]: null }).base.length;
      expect(full - without).toBe(item.draw(CTX).base!.length);
    }
  });

  it('skips an unknown item id instead of throwing', () => {
    expect(() => staticOf({ ...STARTER_LOADOUT, plant: 'no_such_item' })).not.toThrow();
    expect(staticOf({ ...STARTER_LOADOUT, plant: 'no_such_item' })).toEqual(staticOf({ ...STARTER_LOADOUT, plant: null }));
  });

  it('keeps the window, the desk and KIP inside the focus box', () => {
    const d = drawRoom(INPUT);
    expect(boxContains(FOCUS_BOX, boundsOf(d.kip)!)).toBe(true);
    expect(boxContains(FOCUS_BOX, boundsOf(itemArt('monitor')!.draw(CTX).base!)!)).toBe(true);
    expect(boxContains(FOCUS_BOX, boundsOf(itemArt('plain_frame')!.draw(CTX).base!)!)).toBe(true);
    expect([d.focus, d.thumb]).toEqual([FOCUS_BOX, THUMB_BOX]);
  });

  it('keeps KIP (every pose, mood and frame) and the monitor glass inside the thumbnail box', () => {
    for (const pose of KIP_POSES) for (const mood of KIP_MOODS) for (const frame of [0, 1, 2]) {
      const b = boundsOf(drawKipLayer({ ...KIP, pose, mood, frame }, SEATS.standard))!;
      expect({ pose, mood, inside: boxContains(THUMB_BOX, b) }).toEqual({ pose, mood, inside: true });
    }
    expect(boxContains(THUMB_BOX, MONITOR_GLASS)).toBe(true);
  });

  it.each(WEATHERS)('%s: night veil only at night, rain only when stormy, sun only in clear, mostly and short', (weather) => {
    const day = staticOf(STARTER_LOADOUT, weather, false);
    const night = staticOf(STARTER_LOADOUT, weather, true);
    expect(has(day.light, NIGHT_VEIL)).toBe(false);
    expect(has(night.light, NIGHT_VEIL)).toBe(true);
    expect(has(day.base, RAIN)).toBe(weather === 'stormy');
    expect(has(night.base, RAIN)).toBe(false);
    expect(has(day.base, SUN)).toBe(['clear', 'mostly', 'short'].includes(weather));
    expect(has(night.base, SUN)).toBe(false);
  });

  it('has an emissive layer only from emissive items (the lamp and the monitor)', () => {
    expect(staticOf(STARTER_LOADOUT).emissive.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(staticOf({ ...STARTER_LOADOUT, lamp: null, desk: null }).emissive).toEqual([]);
    for (const item of STARTER_ITEMS.filter((i) => !i.emissive)) expect(item.draw(CTX).emissive ?? []).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('takes the seat from the desk item\'s style', () => {
    expect(kipSeatFor(STARTER_LOADOUT)).toEqual(SEATS.standard);
    expect(kipSeatFor({ ...STARTER_LOADOUT, desk: null })).toEqual(SEATS.standard);
    expect(drawRoom(INPUT).kipSeat).toEqual(SEATS.standard);
  });

  it('mirrors KIP while it types at the desk on its left, and not when it faces you', () => {
    const typing = drawKipLayer({ ...KIP, pose: 'type', mood: 'glance' }, SEATS.standard);
    const facingYou = drawKipLayer({ ...KIP, pose: 'type', mood: 'glance', facing: 'you' }, SEATS.standard);
    expect(typing).not.toEqual(facingYou);
    expect(boundsOf(typing)).toEqual(boundsOf(facingYou));
  });

  it('tints the mug in KIP\'s colour', () => {
    for (const colour of ['coral', 'cobalt', 'lime'] as const) {
      const ramp = kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS[colour]);
      expect(has(itemArt('mug')!.draw({ ...CTX, colour, ramp }).base!, ramp.body)).toBe(true);
    }
    expect(itemArt('mug')!.tint).toBe(true);
  });

  it('puts the monitor\'s screen in the loop frames, never in the static layers', () => {
    const frames = (screen: 'lines' | 'dots' | 'chart') => drawRoomFrames({ loadout: STARTER_LOADOUT, weather: 'cloudy', night: false, colour: 'coral', screen });
    expect(frames('dots')).not.toEqual(frames('chart'));
    expect(frames('dots')).not.toEqual(frames('lines'));
    expect(drawRoom({ ...INPUT, screen: 'dots' }).base).toEqual(drawRoom({ ...INPUT, screen: 'chart' }).base);
    const [f0, f1] = frames('dots');
    expect(f0).not.toEqual(f1);
  });
});

describe('memo keys', () => {
  it('the picture key changes with loadout, weather, night and colour, and nothing else', () => {
    const base = { loadout: STARTER_LOADOUT, weather: 'cloudy' as const, night: false, colour: 'coral' as const };
    const key = roomPictureKey(base);
    expect(roomPictureKey({ ...base })).toBe(key);
    for (const changed of [{ loadout: { ...STARTER_LOADOUT, plant: null } }, { weather: 'clear' as const }, { night: true }, { colour: 'mint' as const }]) {
      expect(roomPictureKey({ ...base, ...changed })).not.toBe(key);
    }
  });

  it('the KIP key changes with colour, pose, mood, worn and frame', () => {
    const key = kipLayerKey(KIP);
    for (const changed of [{ colour: 'mint' as const }, { pose: 'type' as const }, { mood: 'glance' as const }, { worn: { ...EMPTY_WORN, head: 'beanie' } }, { frame: 1 }]) {
      expect(kipLayerKey({ ...KIP, ...changed })).not.toBe(key);
    }
  });
});

describe('the window from Home\'s scene', () => {
  it('maps every scene key, with night as its own flag', () => {
    expect(roomWeatherOf('night')).toEqual({ weather: 'fallback', night: true });
    for (const key of ['clear', 'mostly', 'cloudy', 'stormy', 'short', 'fallback'] as const) expect(roomWeatherOf(key)).toEqual({ weather: key, night: false });
  });
});

it('perf: logs how long the starter room takes to build (spec §5 budget: 8 ms on device, release build)', () => {
  const start = performance.now();
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) drawRoom(INPUT);
  const ms = (performance.now() - start) / 10;
  // Logs the duration only; the device budget is checked in Task 16 on the simulator.
  console.info(`drawRoom: ${ms.toFixed(2)} ms`);
  expect(ms).toBeLessThan(50);
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The grid and the paint helpers**

Create `mobile/src/lib/room/grid.ts`:

```ts
// KIP's room grid (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §3.2, §5): 170 × 110 cells, drawn at 2 points a cell in the diorama
// and 1 in the collapsed header's thumbnail. One-point perspective: the back wall runs x 10–160 down to the floor
// line, and the side walls and floor seams converge on the vanishing point. Ported from the v2 engine
// (docs/design/kip-coach/project/lib2.js).
export const ROOM_W = 170;
export const ROOM_H = 110;
export const CELL_PT = 2;
export const FLOOR_Y = 74;
export const VANISH_X = 85;
export const VANISH_Y = 20;
export const WALL_X0 = 10;
export const WALL_X1 = 160;

export interface Box {
  x: number;
  y: number;
  w: number;
  h: number;
}

/** One rect on the grid: x, y, w, h (cells) and a CSS colour ('#RRGGBB' or 'rgba(…)'). */
export type RoomRect = [number, number, number, number, string];

/** The window, the desk and KIP: always on screen, centred by the crop (§3.2). 280 × 200 points at 2 pt. */
export const FOCUS_BOX: Box = { x: 15, y: 10, w: 140, h: 100 };
/** KIP and the monitor: the collapsed header's thumbnail (§3.5), 96 × 60 points at 1 pt. */
export const THUMB_BOX: Box = { x: 70, y: 40, w: 96, h: 60 };

/** The smallest box holding every rect, or null for none. */
export function boundsOf(rects: readonly RoomRect[]): Box | null {
  if (!rects.length) return null;
  let x0 = Infinity, y0 = Infinity, x1 = -Infinity, y1 = -Infinity;
  for (const [x, y, w, h] of rects) {
    x0 = Math.min(x0, x); y0 = Math.min(y0, y); x1 = Math.max(x1, x + w); y1 = Math.max(y1, y + h);
  }
  return { x: x0, y: y0, w: x1 - x0, h: y1 - y0 };
}

export function boxContains(outer: Box, inner: Box): boolean {
  return inner.x >= outer.x && inner.y >= outer.y && inner.x + inner.w <= outer.x + outer.w && inner.y + inner.h <= outer.y + outer.h;
}
```

Create `mobile/src/lib/room/paint.ts`. This is a typed port of `lib.js` (`R`, `Rc`, `poly`) and `lib2.js` (`E`, `glow`,
`shade`, `gradV`, `clipB`, `leaf`, `pot`, `book`, `bookRow`, `seamX`, `floorSeams`, `ao`):

```ts
// The v2 room engine's drawing helpers (docs/design/kip-coach/project/lib.js and lib2.js), typed. Every helper
// appends whole-cell rects to `r`; nothing here knows about items or KIP.
import { darkenHex, mixHex } from '../colour';
import { FLOOR_Y, ROOM_H, ROOM_W, VANISH_X, VANISH_Y, WALL_X0, WALL_X1, type RoomRect } from './grid';

export type Rects = RoomRect[];

export function R(r: Rects, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, c: string): void {
  if (w > 0 && h > 0) r.push([x, y, w, h, c]);
}

export function px(r: Rects, x: number, y: number, c: string): void {
  R(r, x, y, 1, 1, c);
}

/** A rect clipped to box [x, y, w, h]. */
export function Rc(r: Rects, b: readonly [number, number, number, number], x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, c: string): void {
  const x0 = Math.max(x, b[0]), y0 = Math.max(y, b[1]), x1 = Math.min(x + w, b[0] + b[2]), y1 = Math.min(y + h, b[1] + b[3]);
  R(r, x0, y0, x1 - x0, y1 - y0, c);
}

/** A convex polygon, filled column by column. */
export function poly(r: Rects, pts: readonly (readonly [number, number])[], c: string): void {
  let minX = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity;
  for (const p of pts) { minX = Math.min(minX, p[0]); maxX = Math.max(maxX, p[0]); }
  for (let x = Math.round(minX); x < Math.round(maxX); x++) {
    const cx = x + 0.5;
    let lo = Infinity, hi = -Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < pts.length; i++) {
      const a = pts[i]!, b = pts[(i + 1) % pts.length]!;
      if ((a[0] <= cx && b[0] > cx) || (b[0] <= cx && a[0] > cx)) {
        const y = a[1] + ((cx - a[0]) / (b[0] - a[0])) * (b[1] - a[1]);
        lo = Math.min(lo, y); hi = Math.max(hi, y);
      }
    }
    if (hi > lo) {
      const y0 = Math.round(lo), y1 = Math.round(hi);
      if (y1 > y0) R(r, x, y0, 1, y1 - y0, c);
    }
  }
}

/** A filled ellipse, row by row. */
export function E(r: Rects, cx: number, cy: number, rx: number, ry: number, c: string): void {
  for (let y = Math.floor(cy - ry); y < Math.ceil(cy + ry); y++) {
    const dy = (y + 0.5 - cy) / ry;
    if (Math.abs(dy) >= 1) continue;
    const hw = rx * Math.sqrt(1 - dy * dy), x0 = Math.round(cx - hw), x1 = Math.round(cx + hw);
    if (x1 > x0) R(r, x0, y, x1 - x0, 1, c);
  }
}

/** A soft glow: four stacked ellipses of one translucent colour. `rgb` is "r,g,b". */
export function glow(r: Rects, cx: number, cy: number, rx: number, ry: number, rgb: string, a: number): void {
  for (const k of [1, 0.78, 0.56, 0.34]) E(r, cx, cy, rx * k, ry * k, `rgba(${rgb},${a})`);
}

/** A soft contact shadow under furniture. */
export function shade(r: Rects, cx: number, cy: number, rx: number, ry: number, a = 0.16): void {
  E(r, cx, cy, rx, ry, `rgba(0,0,0,${a})`);
  E(r, cx, cy, rx * 0.72, ry * 0.62, `rgba(0,0,0,${a})`);
}

/** A vertical gradient of `cols` bands with ordered-dither seams between them. */
export function gradV(r: Rects, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, cols: readonly string[]): void {
  const n = cols.length;
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
    const y0 = Math.round(y + (i * h) / n), y1 = Math.round(y + ((i + 1) * h) / n);
    R(r, x, y0, w, y1 - y0, cols[i]!);
  }
  for (let i = 1; i < n; i++) {
    const yb = Math.round(y + (i * h) / n);
    for (let xx = x; xx < x + w; xx += 2) px(r, xx, yb, cols[i - 1]!);
    for (let xx = x + 1; xx < x + w; xx += 4) px(r, xx, yb + 1, cols[i - 1]!);
    for (let xx = x + 3; xx < x + w; xx += 4) px(r, xx, yb - 1, cols[i]!);
  }
}

/** Rects clipped to box b. */
export function clipB(rs: readonly RoomRect[], b: readonly [number, number, number, number]): Rects {
  const o: Rects = [];
  for (const q of rs) {
    const x0 = Math.max(q[0], b[0]), y0 = Math.max(q[1], b[1]), x1 = Math.min(q[0] + q[2], b[0] + b[2]), y1 = Math.min(q[1] + q[3], b[1] + b[3]);
    if (x1 > x0 && y1 > y0) o.push([x0, y0, x1 - x0, y1 - y0, q[4]]);
  }
  return o;
}

/** A leaf from base (bx, by) to tip (tx, ty), `w` wide at the middle, with an optional lit half. */
export function leaf(r: Rects, bx: number, by: number, tx: number, ty: number, w: number, c: string, hl?: string): void {
  const mx = (bx + tx) / 2, my = (by + ty) / 2, L = Math.hypot(tx - bx, ty - by) || 1;
  const nx = (-(ty - by) / L) * w, ny = ((tx - bx) / L) * w;
  poly(r, [[bx, by], [mx + nx, my + ny], [tx, ty], [mx - nx, my - ny]], c);
  if (hl) poly(r, [[bx, by], [mx + nx * 0.6, my + ny * 0.6], [tx, ty], [mx, my]], hl);
}

/** A tapered pot standing on yb, with a rim and a shaded right side. */
export function pot(r: Rects, cx: number, yb: number, w: number, h: number, c: string, rim?: string): void {
  poly(r, [[cx - w / 2, yb - h], [cx + w / 2, yb - h], [cx + w / 2 - 2, yb], [cx - w / 2 + 2, yb]], c);
  R(r, Math.round(cx - w / 2 - 1), yb - h - 2, w + 2, 2, rim ?? mixHex(c, '#FFFFFF', 0.2));
  poly(r, [[cx + w / 2 - 3, yb - h], [cx + w / 2, yb - h], [cx + w / 2 - 2, yb], [cx + w / 2 - 4, yb]], darkenHex(c, 0.78));
}

/** One book standing on yb, with a lit top, a shaded spine edge and two bands when tall enough. */
export function book(r: Rects, x: number, yb: number, w: number, h: number, c: string): void {
  R(r, x, yb - h, w, h, c);
  R(r, x, yb - h, w, 1, mixHex(c, '#FFFFFF', 0.28));
  R(r, x + w - 1, yb - h, 1, h, darkenHex(c, 0.72));
  if (h > 6) {
    R(r, x, yb - h + 2, w - 1, 1, mixHex(c, '#F5E6B8', 0.45));
    R(r, x, yb - 3, w - 1, 1, mixHex(c, '#F5E6B8', 0.45));
  }
}

/** A row of books from x to x1 on yb, varied by `seed`, with the odd leaning one. */
export function bookRow(r: Rects, x: number, yb: number, x1: number, cols: readonly string[], seed: number, hmax = 11): void {
  let i = 0;
  while (x < x1 - 2) {
    const w = 2 + ((seed + i * 7) % 3 === 0 ? 1 : 0), h = Math.min(hmax, hmax - 4 + ((seed + i * 5) % 5));
    if (x + w > x1) break;
    book(r, x, yb, w, h, cols[(seed + i * 3) % cols.length]!);
    x += w;
    i++;
    if ((seed + i) % 11 === 0 && x < x1 - 5) {
      poly(r, [[x, yb], [x + 2, yb], [x + 5, yb - h + 2], [x + 3, yb - h + 1]], cols[(i + 2) % cols.length]!);
      x += 5;
    }
  }
}

/** Where a back-wall x on the floor line meets the front edge, along the perspective. */
export function seamX(xb: number): number {
  return VANISH_X + ((xb - VANISH_X) * (ROOM_H - VANISH_Y)) / (FLOOR_Y - VANISH_Y);
}

/** Floor boards: perspective seams every `step` along the back wall, and cross seams at rows `ys`. */
export function floorSeams(r: Rects, step: number, c: string, ys: readonly number[] = []): void {
  for (let xb = WALL_X0 + step; xb < WALL_X1; xb += step) {
    const xf = seamX(xb);
    poly(r, [[xb, FLOOR_Y], [xb + 1, FLOOR_Y], [xf + 1, ROOM_H], [xf, ROOM_H]], c);
  }
  for (const y of ys) {
    const xl = y < FLOOR_Y + 12 ? WALL_X0 - ((y - FLOOR_Y) * WALL_X0) / 12 : 0;
    R(r, Math.round(xl), y, Math.round(ROOM_W - 2 * xl), 1, c);
  }
}

/** Ambient occlusion: the ceiling line, the wall corners, the skirting and the front edge (lib2.js `ao`). */
export function ao(r: Rects): void {
  R(r, 0, 0, ROOM_W, 2, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.22)'); R(r, 0, 2, ROOM_W, 3, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.1)');
  R(r, WALL_X0, 0, 3, FLOOR_Y, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.14)'); R(r, WALL_X0 + 3, 0, 3, FLOOR_Y, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.06)');
  R(r, WALL_X1 - 3, 0, 3, FLOOR_Y, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.14)'); R(r, WALL_X1 - 6, 0, 3, FLOOR_Y, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.06)');
  R(r, WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y - 3, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, 3, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.08)'); R(r, WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y - 1, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, 1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.1)');
  R(r, WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, 2, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.16)'); R(r, WALL_X0 - 2, FLOOR_Y + 2, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0 + 4, 2, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.07)');
  poly(r, [[0, 0], [WALL_X0, 0], [WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y], [0, FLOOR_Y + 12]], 'rgba(0,0,0,0.12)');
  poly(r, [[WALL_X1, 0], [ROOM_W, 0], [ROOM_W, FLOOR_Y + 12], [WALL_X1, FLOOR_Y]], 'rgba(0,0,0,0.18)');
  R(r, 0, ROOM_H - 3, ROOM_W, 3, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.12)');
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Weather, slots, the loadout and the item registry**

Create `mobile/src/lib/room/weather.ts`:

```ts
// The window's sky (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §6), ported from lib2.js `sky2`: smooth sun, puffy clouds, rain on
// the glass, the moon; dithered gradients. The states are Home F's (lib/home/scene.ts SceneKey), with night as its
// own flag so the room can veil itself and light the lamp.
import type { SceneKey } from '../home/scene';
import { ROOM_H, ROOM_W, type Box } from './grid';
import { E, clipB, glow, gradV, poly, px, R, type Rects } from './paint';

export type RoomWeather = 'clear' | 'mostly' | 'cloudy' | 'stormy' | 'short' | 'fallback';

/** Home's scene key → the window: `night` is the moon and the veil; `short` is haze and a small sun. */
export function roomWeatherOf(key: SceneKey): { weather: RoomWeather; night: boolean } {
  return key === 'night' ? { weather: 'fallback', night: true } : { weather: key, night: false };
}

type SkyKey = RoomWeather | 'night';
const SKY: Record<SkyKey, readonly string[]> = {
  clear: ['#3B82F6', '#5596F8', '#71AAFA', '#93C1FC'],
  mostly: ['#3F86F6', '#5998F8', '#76ADFA', '#9CC6FC'],
  short: ['#6C93D8', '#86A6DE', '#A4BCE3', '#C7D3E6'],
  fallback: ['#3F86F6', '#5998F8', '#76ADFA', '#9CC6FC'],
  cloudy: ['#66758D', '#76849B', '#8794AA', '#9EA9BC'],
  stormy: ['#192131', '#202A3A', '#283244', '#323D52'],
  night: ['#060A17', '#0A1122', '#0F1830', '#1A1A45'],
};

/** The sun's disc colour; tests use it to find the sun. */
export const SUN = '#FCD34D';
/** Rain streak colour on the glass. */
export const RAIN = '#8FB4E8';
/** The night veil over the whole room (light layer). */
export const NIGHT_VEIL = 'rgba(6,8,24,0.56)';

function puff(t: Rects, cx: number, cy: number, s: number, base: string, hl: string, sh: string): void {
  E(t, cx, cy + s * 0.35, s * 1.7, s * 0.5, sh);
  E(t, cx - s * 0.7, cy + s * 0.05, s * 0.75, s * 0.6, base);
  E(t, cx + s * 0.6, cy, s * 0.85, s * 0.68, base);
  E(t, cx - s * 0.05, cy - s * 0.35, s * 0.7, s * 0.66, base);
  E(t, cx - s * 0.2, cy - s * 0.7, s * 0.4, s * 0.22, hl);
}

/** The sky inside box b, clipped to it. */
export function windowSky(r: Rects, b: Box, weather: RoomWeather, night: boolean): void {
  const t: Rects = [];
  const { x, y, w, h } = b;
  const key: SkyKey = night ? 'night' : weather;
  const s = Math.max(4, Math.round(h * 0.14));
  gradV(t, x, y, w, h, SKY[key]);
  const sx = x + w * 0.72, sy = y + h * 0.27;
  if (key === 'clear' || key === 'mostly') {
    glow(t, sx, sy, s * 2.8, s * 2.8, '253,230,138', 0.1);
    E(t, sx, sy, s, s, SUN);
    E(t, sx - s * 0.25, sy - s * 0.25, s * 0.62, s * 0.62, '#FDE68A');
  }
  if (key === 'mostly') puff(t, x + w * 0.3, y + h * 0.62, s * 1.1, '#F3F5F9', '#FFFFFF', '#D6DCE6');
  if (key === 'short') {
    // A short night: a small sun low in a hazy sky.
    E(t, sx, y + h * 0.4, s * 0.6, s * 0.6, SUN);
    for (let yy = Math.round(y + h * 0.55); yy < y + h; yy += 3) R(t, x, yy, w, 1, 'rgba(255,244,214,0.28)');
  }
  if (key === 'fallback') puff(t, x + w * 0.4, y + h * 0.55, s, '#F3F5F9', '#FFFFFF', '#D6DCE6');
  if (key === 'cloudy') {
    E(t, sx, sy, s * 0.8, s * 0.8, '#E9D9A6');
    puff(t, x + w * 0.28, y + h * 0.36, s * 1.2, '#DCE1E8', '#EEF1F5', '#B9C1CD');
    puff(t, x + w * 0.74, y + h * 0.56, s * 1.3, '#C7CFDA', '#DCE1E8', '#A9B3C2');
    puff(t, x + w * 0.2, y + h * 0.84, s, '#BAC3D0', '#CDD4DE', '#A0AABA');
  }
  if (key === 'stormy') {
    puff(t, x + w * 0.3, y + h * 0.22, s * 1.5, '#2A3446', '#3A465C', '#1A2130');
    puff(t, x + w * 0.76, y + h * 0.3, s * 1.4, '#252F40', '#354157', '#161C29');
    for (let i = 0; i < w; i += 3) {
      const yy = Math.round(y + h * 0.45 + ((i * 7) % 11));
      px(t, x + i, yy, RAIN);
      px(t, x + i + 1, yy + 1, RAIN);
    }
    poly(t, [[x + w * 0.56, y + h * 0.38], [x + w * 0.6, y + h * 0.38], [x + w * 0.55, y + h * 0.56], [x + w * 0.58, y + h * 0.56],
      [x + w * 0.5, y + h * 0.78], [x + w * 0.52, y + h * 0.58], [x + w * 0.49, y + h * 0.58]], '#FDE047');
  }
  if (key === 'night') {
    glow(t, sx, sy, s * 2.4, s * 2.4, '226,232,240', 0.06);
    E(t, sx, sy, s, s, '#EEF2F7');
    E(t, sx + s * 0.35, sy - s * 0.2, s * 0.8, s * 0.8, SKY.night[1]!);
    for (const [qx, qy] of [[0.12, 0.2], [0.3, 0.12], [0.22, 0.55], [0.45, 0.35], [0.86, 0.7], [0.6, 0.62], [0.08, 0.8], [0.4, 0.82]] as const) {
      px(t, Math.round(x + w * qx), Math.round(y + h * qy), '#F8FAFC');
    }
  }
  for (const q of clipB(t, [x, y, w, h])) r.push(q);
}

const DAYLIGHT: Record<RoomWeather, number> = { clear: 0.11, mostly: 0.09, short: 0.07, fallback: 0.07, cloudy: 0.05, stormy: 0.03 };

/** Daylight falling from the window onto the floor (by weather), from window x0..x1 whose sill is at ybot. */
export function daylight(r: Rects, weather: RoomWeather, x0: number, x1: number, ybot: number): void {
  const a = DAYLIGHT[weather];
  poly(r, [[x0 + 2, ybot], [x1 + 2, ybot], [x1 + 30, ROOM_H], [x0 + 12, ROOM_H]], `rgba(255,241,210,${a})`);
  poly(r, [[x0 + 6, ybot + 6], [x1 - 2, ybot + 6], [x1 + 20, ROOM_H], [x0 + 16, ROOM_H]], `rgba(255,241,210,${(a * 0.6).toFixed(3)})`);
}

export function nightVeil(r: Rects): void {
  R(r, 0, 0, ROOM_W, ROOM_H, NIGHT_VEIL);
}
```

// The room's slots (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §5). Spec B (KIP's room) uses the same ids for its catalogue and its
// decorate-mode hotspots, so these names are fixed. Each room slot has an anchor rect on the 170 × 110 grid: where
// its item sits and where Spec B puts the slot's hotspot.
import type { Box } from './grid';

export const ROOM_SLOTS = ['wallpaper', 'floor', 'art', 'window', 'shelf', 'desk', 'desk_item', 'plant', 'lamp', 'corner', 'rug', 'pet'] as const;
export type RoomSlot = (typeof ROOM_SLOTS)[number];

export const WORN_SLOTS = ['head', 'face', 'neck', 'ears'] as const;
export type WornSlot = (typeof WORN_SLOTS)[number];

export const SLOT_ANCHORS: Record<RoomSlot, Box> = {
  wallpaper: { x: 0, y: 0, w: 170, h: 86 },
  floor: { x: 0, y: 74, w: 170, h: 36 },
  window: { x: 16, y: 12, w: 40, h: 43 },
  art: { x: 130, y: 12, w: 22, h: 24 },
  shelf: { x: 94, y: 16, w: 38, h: 18 },
  desk: { x: 58, y: 46, w: 84, h: 56 },
  desk_item: { x: 102, y: 56, w: 8, h: 18 },
  plant: { x: 148, y: 74, w: 22, h: 36 },
  lamp: { x: 58, y: 50, w: 16, h: 22 },
  corner: { x: 0, y: 30, w: 14, h: 66 },
  rug: { x: 28, y: 90, w: 118, h: 18 },
  pet: { x: 30, y: 92, w: 26, h: 14 },
};

/** Back to front: walls, floor, wall pieces, floor pieces, the desk, what stands on it, then the foreground. */
export const DRAW_ORDER: readonly RoomSlot[] = ['wallpaper', 'floor', 'window', 'art', 'shelf', 'rug', 'corner', 'desk', 'desk_item', 'lamp', 'pet', 'plant'];
