Create `mobile/src/lib/room/loadout.ts`:

```ts
// What fills each slot (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §5). Spec A passes STARTER_LOADOUT and EMPTY_WORN; Spec B reads
// the user's loadout and worn items from the server.
import { ROOM_SLOTS, WORN_SLOTS, type RoomSlot, type WornSlot } from './slots';

export type ItemId = string;
export type RoomLoadout = Record<RoomSlot, ItemId | null>;
export type KipWorn = Record<WornSlot, ItemId | null>;

/** K4's furnishing at v2 quality (§5 "The starter furnishing"). */
export const STARTER_LOADOUT: RoomLoadout = {
  wallpaper: 'plain_slate',
  floor: 'dark_oak',
  art: 'wall_calendar',
  window: 'plain_frame',
  shelf: 'books',
  desk: 'monitor',
  desk_item: 'mug',
  plant: 'fern',
  lamp: 'amber_lamp',
  corner: null,
  rug: 'slate_rug',
  pet: null,
};

/** KIP wears nothing in Spec A. */
export const EMPTY_WORN: KipWorn = { head: null, face: null, neck: null, ears: null };

/** A stable string for memo keys: every slot in a fixed order. */
export function loadoutKey(loadout: RoomLoadout): string {
  return ROOM_SLOTS.map((s) => `${s}=${loadout[s] ?? ''}`).join('|');
}

export function wornKey(worn: KipWorn): string {
  return WORN_SLOTS.map((s) => `${s}=${worn[s] ?? ''}`).join('|');
}
```

Create `mobile/src/lib/room/items.ts`:

```ts
// Room item art (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §5): one registry of art by item id. Every item fills exactly one slot.
// Spec A registers the ten placed starter items (starterItems.ts); Spec B registers the rest of its catalogue
// with registerItems(), and never changes this contract.
import type { KipColour, KipRamp } from '../kip/kipArt';
import type { RoomRect } from './grid';
import type { ItemId } from './loadout';
import type { RoomSlot } from './slots';
import type { RoomWeather } from './weather';

/** What the monitor shows: lines idle, dots while thinking, the chart while answering. */
export type MonitorScreen = 'lines' | 'dots' | 'chart';

export interface ItemContext {
  weather: RoomWeather;
  night: boolean;
  colour: KipColour;
  /** KIP's colour ramp, for tinted items (`tint: true`). */
  ramp: KipRamp;
  /** Only the loop frames may read it: the static picture is memoised without it. */
  screen: MonitorScreen;
}

export interface ItemLayers {
  base?: RoomRect[];
  /** Glows drawn over the light layer (lamps, screens). Only items with `emissive: true` return any. */
  emissive?: RoomRect[];
  /** Three loop frames, 400 ms each. Only items with `frames: 3` return them. */
  frames?: [RoomRect[], RoomRect[], RoomRect[]];
}

export type SeatStyle = 'standard' | 'low';

export interface ItemArt {
  id: ItemId;
  slot: RoomSlot;
  draw(ctx: ItemContext): ItemLayers;
  emissive?: boolean;
  frames?: 3;
  /** Takes KIP's colour (the mug). */
  tint?: boolean;
  /** Desk items only: where KIP sits (draw.ts `kipSeat`). */
  style?: SeatStyle;
}

const REGISTRY = new Map<ItemId, ItemArt>();

export function registerItems(items: readonly ItemArt[]): void {
  for (const item of items) REGISTRY.set(item.id, item);
}

/** The art for an id, or undefined: an unknown id is skipped by the engine, never thrown. */
export function itemArt(id: ItemId | null | undefined): ItemArt | undefined {
  return id ? REGISTRY.get(id) : undefined;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: The starter furnishing**

Create `mobile/src/lib/room/starterItems.ts`. Each item draws only into its own slot's anchor; the test checks that.

```ts
// The ten placed starter items (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §5): K4's furnishing (docs/design/kip-coach/project/
// K4.dc.html, k4room.js) redrawn on the 170 × 110 grid at Theme sets v2 quality (SetsV2.dc.html, sets2.js):
// perspective walls, lighting, dithered gradients and soft shadows. Plan ruling 2: the desk and monitor sit on
// KIP's left, so KIP faces the monitor mirrored, glancing toward it.
import { darkenHex, mixHex } from '../colour';
import { FLOOR_Y, ROOM_H, ROOM_W, WALL_X0, WALL_X1 } from './grid';
import { registerItems, type ItemArt, type MonitorScreen } from './items';
import { bookRow, clipB, E, floorSeams, glow, gradV, leaf, poly, pot, px, R, shade, type Rects } from './paint';
import { windowSky } from './weather';

const WOOD = { leg: '#7C5A3E', top: '#9A7350' };
const CHAIR = '#3F4656';
const BOOKS = ['#60A5FA', '#F87171', '#FDE047', '#475569', '#A78BFA', '#34D399'];

/** The monitor's glass, inside its bezel: the loop frames draw the screen content here. */
export const MONITOR_GLASS = { x: 74, y: 50, w: 24, h: 14 } as const;

const three = (f: (k: 0 | 1 | 2) => Rects): [Rects, Rects, Rects] => [f(0), f(1), f(2)];

const plainSlate: ItemArt = {
  id: 'plain_slate',
  slot: 'wallpaper',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    gradV(r, WALL_X0, 0, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y, ['#323A4B', '#2E3646', '#2A3140', '#262D3B']);
    poly(r, [[0, 0], [WALL_X0, 0], [WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y], [0, FLOOR_Y + 12]], '#232A37');
    poly(r, [[WALL_X1, 0], [ROOM_W, 0], [ROOM_W, FLOOR_Y + 12], [WALL_X1, FLOOR_Y]], '#232A37');
    // K4's panel line and wainscot.
    R(r, WALL_X0, 56, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, 1, '#3A4357');
    for (let x = WALL_X0 + 4; x < WALL_X1; x += 12) R(r, x, 58, 1, 14, '#2F3747');
    R(r, WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y - 2, WALL_X1 - WALL_X0, 2, '#1E2430');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

const darkOak: ItemArt = {
  id: 'dark_oak',
  slot: 'floor',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    poly(r, [[WALL_X0, FLOOR_Y], [WALL_X1, FLOOR_Y], [ROOM_W, FLOOR_Y + 12], [ROOM_W, ROOM_H], [0, ROOM_H], [0, FLOOR_Y + 12]], '#5B4636');
    floorSeams(r, 10, '#4A382B', [80, 88, 98]);
    // The outer seams run past the front corners in perspective: keep them on the grid.
    return { base: clipB(r, [0, 0, ROOM_W, ROOM_H]) };
  },
};

const plainFrame: ItemArt = {
  id: 'plain_frame',
  slot: 'window',
  draw: ({ weather, night }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    R(r, 18, 12, 36, 40, '#1E2430');
    windowSky(r, { x: 20, y: 14, w: 32, h: 36 }, weather, night);
    R(r, 35, 14, 2, 36, '#1E2430');
    R(r, 20, 31, 32, 1, '#1E2430');
    R(r, 16, 52, 40, 3, '#191E28');
    R(r, 16, 52, 40, 1, '#2A3140');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

const wallCalendar: ItemArt = {
  id: 'wall_calendar',
  slot: 'art',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    R(r, 140, 12, 2, 2, '#94A3B8');
    R(r, 132, 14, 18, 20, '#1F2937');
    R(r, 133, 15, 16, 18, '#F1F5F9');
    R(r, 133, 15, 16, 4, '#DC2626');
    R(r, 133, 15, 16, 1, '#F87171');
    for (let row = 0; row < 4; row++) for (let col = 0; col < 5; col++) px(r, 134 + col * 3, 21 + row * 3, '#94A3B8');
    R(r, 140, 24, 2, 2, '#DC2626');
    R(r, 133, 32, 16, 1, '#CBD5E1');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

const books: ItemArt = {
  id: 'books',
  slot: 'shelf',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    shade(r, 113, 33, 18, 1.5, 0.12);
    bookRow(r, 98, 30, 128, BOOKS, 3, 10);
    R(r, 96, 30, 34, 2, '#5B4636');
    R(r, 96, 30, 34, 1, '#7C5A3E');
    R(r, 99, 32, 1, 2, '#3A2D23');
    R(r, 126, 32, 1, 2, '#3A2D23');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

const slateRug: ItemArt = {
  id: 'slate_rug',
  slot: 'rug',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    poly(r, [[40, 92], [134, 92], [144, 106], [30, 106]], '#475569');
    poly(r, [[43, 94], [131, 94], [139, 104], [35, 104]], '#334155');
    poly(r, [[45, 95], [129, 95], [136, 103], [38, 103]], '#475569');
    for (let x = 50; x < 128; x += 8) E(r, x, 99, 1.6, 1, '#3E4A5C');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

function screenContent(screen: MonitorScreen, k: 0 | 1 | 2, accent: string): Rects {
  const r: Rects = [];
  const { x, y } = MONITOR_GLASS;
  const L = '#7DD3FC';
  if (screen === 'dots') {
    // The thinking dots: the lit one moves along.
    [5, 10, 15].forEach((dx, i) => R(r, x + dx, y + 6, 2, 2, i === k ? accent : mixHex(accent, '#0F1A2A', 0.55)));
  } else if (screen === 'chart') {
    ([[3, 3], [6, 5], [9, 2], [12, 6], [15, 4]] as const).forEach(([bx, bh]) => R(r, x + bx, y + 11 - bh, 2, bh, bx === 12 ? accent : L));
    if (k !== 2) R(r, x + 19, y + 3, 1, 2, L);
  } else {
    R(r, x + 2, y + 2, 10, 1, L);
    R(r, x + 2, y + 5, 16, 1, L);
    R(r, x + 2, y + 8, 8, 1, L);
    // A blinking cursor after the last line.
    if (k !== 2) R(r, x + 11, y + 8, 1, 1, L);
  }
  return r;
}

/** A readable accent on the dark glass: ink and slate are too dark, so they show as off-white (lib.js accentOf). */
const accentOn = (body: string) => {
  const n = parseInt(body.slice(1), 16);
  const lum = (0.299 * ((n >> 16) & 255) + 0.587 * ((n >> 8) & 255) + 0.114 * (n & 255)) / 255;
  return lum < 0.42 ? '#F5F5F4' : body;
};

const monitor: ItemArt = {
  id: 'monitor',
  slot: 'desk',
  style: 'standard',
  emissive: true,
  frames: 3,
  draw: ({ night, ramp, screen }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    // The desk (sets2.js `desk`).
    shade(r, 88, 99, 30, 3, 0.12);
    R(r, 64, 75, 3, 24, darkenHex(WOOD.leg, 0.85));
    R(r, 109, 75, 3, 24, darkenHex(WOOD.leg, 0.85));
    R(r, 62, 72, 52, 3, WOOD.top);
    R(r, 62, 72, 52, 1, mixHex(WOOD.top, '#FFFFFF', 0.22));
    R(r, 62, 75, 52, 2, WOOD.leg);
    // KIP's chair, back on the right.
    shade(r, 123, 96, 18, 2);
    R(r, 136, 58, 3, 29, CHAIR);
    R(r, 138, 58, 1, 29, mixHex(CHAIR, '#FFFFFF', 0.15));
    R(r, 108, 84, 31, 3, CHAIR);
    R(r, 108, 84, 31, 1, mixHex(CHAIR, '#FFFFFF', 0.15));
    R(r, 110, 87, 2, 9, darkenHex(CHAIR, 0.8));
    R(r, 134, 87, 2, 9, darkenHex(CHAIR, 0.8));
    // The monitor, its stand and the keyboard.
    R(r, 72, 48, 28, 18, '#1F2937');
    gradV(r, 73, 49, 26, 16, ['#0B1422', '#0F1A2A', '#132036']);
    R(r, 84, 66, 4, 4, '#334155');
    R(r, 80, 70, 12, 2, '#334155');
    R(r, 96, 70, 14, 2, '#475569');
    R(r, 96, 70, 14, 1, '#64748B');
    const emissive: Rects = [];
    glow(emissive, 86, 57, 20, 13, '125,211,252', night ? 0.08 : 0.035);
    return {
      base: r,
      emissive,
      frames: three((k) => screenContent(screen, k, accentOn(ramp.body))),
    };
  },
};

const mug: ItemArt = {
  id: 'mug',
  slot: 'desk_item',
  tint: true,
  frames: 3,
  draw: ({ ramp }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    shade(r, 106, 72, 4, 1, 0.2);
    R(r, 104, 66, 4, 6, ramp.body);
    R(r, 104, 66, 4, 1, ramp.light);
    R(r, 107, 66, 1, 6, ramp.shade);
    R(r, 108, 67, 1, 3, ramp.shade);
    // Steam: three wisps rising one cell a frame.
    const steam = three((k) => {
      const f: Rects = [];
      [[105, 63], [106, 61], [105, 59]].forEach(([sx, sy], i) => px(f, sx! + ((i + k) % 2), sy! - k, 'rgba(241,245,249,0.45)'));
      return f;
    });
    return { base: r, frames: steam };
  },
};

const amberLamp: ItemArt = {
  id: 'amber_lamp',
  slot: 'lamp',
  emissive: true,
  frames: 3,
  draw: ({ night }) => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    const x = 66, yb = 72;
    R(r, x - 3, yb - 2, 8, 2, '#334155');
    R(r, x, yb - 14, 2, 12, '#334155');
    poly(r, [[x - 5, yb - 14], [x + 2, yb - 19], [x + 5, yb - 16], [x - 2, yb - 11]], '#F59E0B');
    poly(r, [[x - 2, yb - 11], [x + 5, yb - 16], [x + 5, yb - 15], [x - 1, yb - 10]], darkenHex('#F59E0B', 0.7));
    const emissive: Rects = [];
    glow(emissive, x - 1, yb - 4, night ? 18 : 10, night ? 11 : 6, '253,224,71', night ? 0.12 : 0.06);
    // A slight flicker of the pool of light.
    const frames = three((k) => {
      const f: Rects = [];
      if (k !== 1) E(f, x - 1, yb - 2, night ? 9 : 5, night ? 3 : 2, `rgba(253,224,71,${k === 0 ? 0.06 : 0.03})`);
      return f;
    });
    return { base: r, emissive, frames };
  },
};

const fern: ItemArt = {
  id: 'fern',
  slot: 'plant',
  draw: () => {
    const r: Rects = [];
    shade(r, 159, 108, 10, 1.5);
    leaf(r, 156, 98, 149, 80, 2.2, '#15803D', '#22C55E');
    leaf(r, 158, 98, 158, 76, 2.4, '#166534', '#4ADE80');
    leaf(r, 160, 98, 167, 82, 2.2, '#15803D', '#22C55E');
    leaf(r, 157, 98, 151, 88, 1.8, '#16A34A', '#86EFAC');
    leaf(r, 161, 98, 166, 90, 1.8, '#16A34A', '#86EFAC');
    pot(r, 158, 108, 12, 9, '#B45309', '#D97706');
    return { base: r };
  },
};

export const STARTER_ITEMS: readonly ItemArt[] = [plainSlate, darkOak, plainFrame, wallCalendar, books, slateRug, monitor, mug, amberLamp, fern];
registerItems(STARTER_ITEMS);
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `drawRoom`**

Create `mobile/src/lib/room/draw.ts`:

```ts
// The room engine's one entry point (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §5). Pure: no React, no Skia. The order is the v2
// engine's (lib2.js `room2`): walls and floor, items back to front, ambient occlusion, then daylight through the
// window (by weather) or the night veil, then emissive glows, then the loop frames. KIP is its own layer, so a
// pose change never redraws the room. RoomView draws base → kip → light → emissive → frames[i] (plan ruling 4).
import { kipGrid, kipRamp, KIP_COLOURS, KIP_GRID, mirrorGrid, type KipColour, type KipMood, type KipPose } from '../kip/kipArt';
import { FOCUS_BOX, THUMB_BOX, type Box, type RoomRect } from './grid';
import { itemArt, type ItemContext, type MonitorScreen, type SeatStyle } from './items';
import { loadoutKey, wornKey, type KipWorn, type RoomLoadout } from './loadout';
import { ao, R, type Rects } from './paint';
import { DRAW_ORDER, SLOT_ANCHORS } from './slots';
import './starterItems';
import { daylight, nightVeil, type RoomWeather } from './weather';

/** Room cells per KIP pixel: KIP is 32 × 32 cells, 64 × 64 points in the diorama (§4). */
export const KIP_CELLS = 2;

export interface KipSeat {
  /** Top-left of KIP's 16 × 16 grid, in room cells. */
  x: number;
  y: number;
  /** Which side of KIP the desk is on; "facing the desk" mirrors KIP when it is on the left (§4). */
  deskSide: 'left' | 'right';
  style: SeatStyle;
}

/** Where KIP sits for each desk style. `low` (floor at a low table) is Spec B's; it may tune it. */
export const SEATS: Record<SeatStyle, KipSeat> = {
  standard: { x: 110, y: 52, deskSide: 'left', style: 'standard' },
  low: { x: 106, y: 49, deskSide: 'left', style: 'low' },
};

export interface RoomKip {
  colour: KipColour;
  pose: KipPose;
  mood: KipMood;
  /** All null in Spec A; Spec B draws worn items. */
  worn: KipWorn;
  /** The loop frame (0–2) for looping poses. */
  frame?: number;
  /** Default: 'desk' for `type`, 'you' otherwise (plan ruling 6). */
  facing?: 'desk' | 'you';
}

export interface RoomInput {
  loadout: RoomLoadout;
  weather: RoomWeather;
  night: boolean;
  kip: RoomKip;
  screen: MonitorScreen;
}

export interface RoomDrawing {
  base: RoomRect[];
  light: RoomRect[];
  emissive: RoomRect[];
  frames: [RoomRect[], RoomRect[], RoomRect[]];
  kip: RoomRect[];
  kipSeat: KipSeat;
  focus: Box;
  thumb: Box;
}

type StaticInput = Pick<RoomInput, 'loadout' | 'weather' | 'night'> & { colour: KipColour };

function context(input: StaticInput, screen: MonitorScreen): ItemContext {
  return { weather: input.weather, night: input.night, colour: input.colour, ramp: kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS[input.colour]), screen };
}

/** The seat from the desk item's style; standard when the desk slot is empty or unknown. */
export function kipSeatFor(loadout: RoomLoadout): KipSeat {
  return SEATS[itemArt(loadout.desk)?.style ?? 'standard'];
}

/** Everything that never changes with KIP's pose or the loop: memoised by roomPictureKey. */
export function drawRoomStatic(input: StaticInput): Pick<RoomDrawing, 'base' | 'light' | 'emissive' | 'kipSeat'> {
  const ctx = context(input, 'lines');
  const base: Rects = [];
  const emissive: Rects = [];
  for (const slot of DRAW_ORDER) {
    const art = itemArt(input.loadout[slot]);
    if (!art || art.slot !== slot) continue;
    const layers = art.draw(ctx);
    if (layers.base) base.push(...layers.base);
    if (art.emissive && layers.emissive) emissive.push(...layers.emissive);
  }
  const light: Rects = [];
  ao(light);
  if (input.night) nightVeil(light);
  else if (itemArt(input.loadout.window)) {
    const w = SLOT_ANCHORS.window;
    daylight(light, input.weather, w.x + 4, w.x + w.w - 4, w.y + w.h - 3);
  }
  return { base, light, emissive, kipSeat: kipSeatFor(input.loadout) };
}

/** The three loop frames (the monitor's screen, steam, the lamp's flicker). */
export function drawRoomFrames(input: StaticInput & { screen: MonitorScreen }): [Rects, Rects, Rects] {
  const ctx = context(input, input.screen);
  const out: [Rects, Rects, Rects] = [[], [], []];
  for (const slot of DRAW_ORDER) {
    const art = itemArt(input.loadout[slot]);
    if (!art || art.slot !== slot || !art.frames) continue;
    const frames = art.draw(ctx).frames;
    if (frames) frames.forEach((f, i) => out[i as 0 | 1 | 2].push(...f));
  }
  return out;
}

/** KIP at its seat, 2 cells per KIP pixel, mirrored when it faces a desk on its left. */
export function drawKipLayer(kip: RoomKip, seat: KipSeat): Rects {
  const facing = kip.facing ?? (kip.pose === 'type' ? 'desk' : 'you');
  let grid = kipGrid({ colour: kip.colour, pose: kip.pose, mood: kip.mood, frame: kip.frame ?? 0 });
  if (facing === 'desk' && seat.deskSide === 'left') grid = mirrorGrid(grid);
  const r: Rects = [];
  for (let y = 0; y < KIP_GRID; y++) {
    let x = 0;
    const row = grid[y]!;
    while (x < KIP_GRID) {
      const c = row[x];
      if (!c) { x++; continue; }
      let w = 1;
      while (x + w < KIP_GRID && row[x + w] === c) w++;
      R(r, seat.x + x * KIP_CELLS, seat.y + y * KIP_CELLS, w * KIP_CELLS, KIP_CELLS, c);
      x += w;
    }
  }
  return r;
}

export function drawRoom(input: RoomInput): RoomDrawing {
  const staticInput = { loadout: input.loadout, weather: input.weather, night: input.night, colour: input.kip.colour };
  const s = drawRoomStatic(staticInput);
  return {
    ...s,
    frames: drawRoomFrames({ ...staticInput, screen: input.screen }),
    kip: drawKipLayer(input.kip, s.kipSeat),
    focus: FOCUS_BOX,
    thumb: THUMB_BOX,
  };
}

/** The static picture's memo key (§5): (loadout, weather, night, colour). */
export function roomPictureKey(input: StaticInput): string {
  return `${loadoutKey(input.loadout)}#${input.weather}#${input.night ? 'n' : 'd'}#${input.colour}`;
}

/** KIP's layer key (§5): (colour, pose, mood, worn, frame), plus facing. */
export function kipLayerKey(kip: RoomKip): string {
  return `${kip.colour}#${kip.pose}#${kip.mood}#${wornKey(kip.worn)}#${kip.frame ?? 0}#${kip.facing ?? ''}`;
}
```

```tsx
import { act, renderHook, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { AppState } from 'react-native';
import { useTodayScene } from '../../src/lib/home/useTodayScene';
import { fetchScoresWithBands } from '../../src/api/scores';
import { fetchSleep } from '../../src/api/sleep';
import { sceneState, todayScore, todayWeather } from '../../src/lib/home/scene';
import { verdictWord } from '../../src/lib/kipCopy';

jest.mock('../../src/api/scores', () => ({ ...jest.requireActual('../../src/api/scores'), fetchScoresWithBands: jest.fn() }));
jest.mock('../../src/api/sleep', () => ({ ...jest.requireActual('../../src/api/sleep'), fetchSleep: jest.fn() }));

const scores = fetchScoresWithBands as jest.Mock;
const sleep = fetchSleep as jest.Mock;
const nav = () => {
  const listeners: Record<string, () => void> = {};
  return { listeners, isFocused: () => true, addListener: (e: string, cb: () => void) => { listeners[e] = cb; return () => delete listeners[e]; } };
};
// 10:00 local on Wed 14 Oct 2026: day, so the window follows the recovery band.
const TEN_AM = new Date(2026, 9, 14, 10, 0, 0);
const RESULT = { scores: [{ type: 'RECOVERY', date: '2026-10-14', score: 58 }], bands: undefined };

beforeEach(() => {
  jest.useFakeTimers({ now: TEN_AM });
  scores.mockResolvedValue(RESULT);
  sleep.mockResolvedValue({ nights: [] });
});
afterEach(() => jest.useRealTimers());

it('gives Home and the KIP page the same state for the same reads', async () => {
  const a = renderHook(() => useTodayScene(nav()));
  const b = renderHook(() => useTodayScene(nav()));
  await waitFor(() => expect(a.result.current.scores.status).toBe('ready'));
  await waitFor(() => expect(b.result.current.scores.status).toBe('ready'));
  expect(a.result.current.scene).toEqual(b.result.current.scene);
  const expected = sceneState({ now: TEN_AM, recovery: todayWeather(todayScore(RESULT.scores as never, 'RECOVERY', '2026-10-14'), undefined), night: null });
  expect(a.result.current.scene).toEqual(expected);
  expect(a.result.current.recovery).toEqual({ score: 58, band: 'scoreFair' });
  expect(verdictWord(a.result.current.recovery.band)).toBe('fair');
});

it('a failed read gives the fallback window and rest / curious, and never throws', async () => {
  scores.mockRejectedValue(new Error('down'));
  sleep.mockRejectedValue(new Error('down'));
  const { result } = renderHook(() => useTodayScene(nav()));
  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.scores.status).toBe('failed'));
  expect(result.current.scene).toMatchObject({ key: 'fallback', pose: 'rest', mood: 'curious' });
  expect(result.current.window).toEqual({ weather: 'fallback', night: false });
  expect(result.current.recovery).toEqual({ score: null, band: null });
});

it('turns to night at 20:00 by itself while focused, with the moon in the window', async () => {
  jest.setSystemTime(new Date(2026, 9, 14, 19, 59, 0));
  const { result } = renderHook(() => useTodayScene(nav()));
  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.scores.status).toBe('ready'));
  expect(result.current.window.night).toBe(false);
  await act(async () => { jest.advanceTimersByTime(61_000); });
  expect(result.current.scene.key).toBe('night');
  expect(result.current.window).toEqual({ weather: 'fallback', night: true });
});

it('re-reads the clock on focus and on return to the foreground', async () => {
  const n = nav();
  const spy = jest.spyOn(AppState, 'addEventListener');
  const { result } = renderHook(() => useTodayScene(n));
  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.scores.status).toBe('ready'));
  jest.setSystemTime(new Date(2026, 9, 14, 21, 0, 0));
  act(() => n.listeners.focus!());
  expect(result.current.scene.key).toBe('night');
  expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith('change', expect.any(Function));
});

it('maps every band to its lower-case verdict word', () => {
  expect(['scoreExcellent', 'scoreGood', 'scoreFair', 'scorePoor'].map((b) => verdictWord(b as never))).toEqual(['excellent', 'good', 'fair', 'low']);
  expect(verdictWord(null)).toBeNull();
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The hook**

Create `mobile/src/lib/home/useTodayScene.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import { AppState } from 'react-native';
import { fetchScoresWithBands, type DailyScoreDTO, type ScoreBandsDTO, type ScoresResult } from '../../api/scores';
import { fetchSleep, type SleepNight } from '../../api/sleep';
import type { Read } from '../../components/home/TodayTiles';
import { useSync } from '../../sync/SyncProvider';
import { todayCivil } from '../heatmap';
import { roomWeatherOf, type RoomWeather } from '../room/weather';
import { scoreBand, type ScoreBand } from '../scoreInsights';
import { nextSceneBoundary, sceneState, todayScore, todayWeather, type SceneState } from './scene';

export interface FocusNavigation {
  isFocused?: () => boolean;
  addListener?: (event: 'focus' | 'blur', callback: () => void) => (() => void) | undefined;
}

export interface TodayScene {
  now: Date;
  today: string;
  focused: boolean;
  scores: Read<ScoresResult>;
  night: Read<SleepNight | null>;
  bands: ScoreBandsDTO | undefined;
  recoveryScore: DailyScoreDTO | null;
  sleepScore: DailyScoreDTO | null;
  lastNight: SleepNight | null;
  scene: SceneState;
  recovery: { score: number | null; band: ScoreBand | null };
  window: { weather: RoomWeather; night: boolean };
}

// Today's scene for Home F and the KIP page's window (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §6): the same reads Home always made
// (fetchScoresWithBands(7), fetchSleep(today, today)), so the window agrees with Home and the Recovery tile. It
// recomputes on focus, on return to the foreground and at the next scene boundary (05:00, 12:00, 18:00, 20:00).
// A failed read gives the fallback state; it never blocks the page.
export function useTodayScene(navigation: FocusNavigation): TodayScene {
  const { dataVersion } = useSync();
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => new Date());
  const [focused, setFocused] = useState<boolean>(() => navigation.isFocused?.() ?? true);
  const [scores, setScores] = useState<Read<ScoresResult>>({ status: 'loading' });
  const [night, setNight] = useState<Read<SleepNight | null>>({ status: 'loading' });
  const today = todayCivil(now);
  const navRef = useRef(navigation);
  navRef.current = navigation;

  // … the four effects moved verbatim from DashboardScreen.tsx:
  //   fetchScoresWithBands(7) keyed [dataVersion, today] (lines 131–137),
  //   fetchSleep(today, today) keyed [dataVersion, today] (lines 139–145),
  //   focus / blur / AppState 'active' → setNow (lines 155–171), with setHomeFocused renamed setFocused,
  //   the boundary timeout keyed [focused, now] (lines 173–177).

  const allScores = scores.status === 'ready' ? scores.value.scores : null;
  const bands = scores.status === 'ready' ? scores.value.bands : undefined;
  const recoveryScore = todayScore(allScores, 'RECOVERY', today);
  const sleepScore = todayScore(allScores, 'SLEEP', today);
  // While a new day's read is in flight, the old day's night must not stand in for it.
  const todaysNight: Read<SleepNight | null> = night.status === 'ready' && night.value && night.value.date !== today ? { status: 'loading' } : night;
  const lastNight = todaysNight.status === 'ready' ? todaysNight.value : null;
  const scene = sceneState({ now, recovery: todayWeather(recoveryScore, bands), night: lastNight });
  const score = recoveryScore && recoveryScore.score !== null ? Math.round(recoveryScore.score) : null;
  return {
    now, today, focused, scores, night: todaysNight, bands, recoveryScore, sleepScore, lastNight, scene,
    recovery: { score, band: score === null ? null : scoreBand(score, bands) },
    window: roomWeatherOf(scene.key),
  };
}
```

Copy the four effects from `DashboardScreen.tsx` with their `cancelled` guards. Rename only `setHomeFocused` to
`setFocused` and `homeFocused` to `focused`. Change nothing else, so Home behaves exactly as before.

In `kipCopy.ts`:

```ts
import { BAND_WORD } from './recoveryCopy';
import type { ScoreBand } from './scoreInsights';

/** "fair": the Recovery page's band word in lower case (§3.3 "{verdict} day"). */
export function verdictWord(band: ScoreBand | null): string | null {
  return band ? BAND_WORD[band].toLowerCase() : null;
}
```

```js
// Jest stand-in for @shopify/react-native-skia (the real module is native). The recap export configures
// makeImageFromView per case. RoomView records pictures with createPicture: the stand-in runs the drawing callback
// against a fake canvas, so tests can count pictures and the colours each one drew.
const React = require('react');
const { View } = require('react-native');

const createPicture = jest.fn((draw) => {
  const colours = [];
  const canvas = { drawPath: (_path, paint) => colours.push(paint.colour), drawRect: (_rect, paint) => colours.push(paint.colour) };
  draw(canvas);
  return { colours, dispose: jest.fn() };
});

const Skia = {
  Paint: () => {
    const paint = { colour: null, setColor: (c) => { paint.colour = c; }, setAntiAlias: jest.fn() };
    return paint;
  },
  Color: (c) => c,
  Path: { Make: () => ({ addRect: jest.fn() }) },
  XYWHRect: (x, y, width, height) => ({ x, y, width, height }),
};

const Canvas = ({ children, style }) => React.createElement(View, { testID: 'skia-canvas', style }, children);
const Group = ({ children, transform }) => React.createElement(View, { testID: 'skia-group', skiaTransform: transform }, children);
const Picture = ({ picture }) => React.createElement(View, { testID: 'skia-picture', picture });

module.exports = { makeImageFromView: jest.fn(), ImageFormat: { JPEG: 3, PNG: 4, WEBP: 6 }, createPicture, Skia, Canvas, Group, Picture };
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The crop**

Create `mobile/src/components/room/roomCrop.ts`:

```ts
import { ROOM_H, ROOM_W, type Box } from '../../lib/room/grid';

// Crop, never shrink (spec §3.2): the box is centred in the view, then the room is clamped so its edges never show.
// Extra width shows more room. Whole points, so the 2-point cells stay on the pixel grid.
export function roomCrop({ width, height, cellPt, box }: { width: number; height: number; cellPt: number; box: Box }): { x: number; y: number } {
  const axis = (view: number, start: number, size: number, room: number) => {
    const roomPt = room * cellPt;
    if (roomPt <= view) return Math.round((view - roomPt) / 2);
    const centred = view / 2 - (start + size / 2) * cellPt;
    return Math.round(Math.min(0, Math.max(view - roomPt, centred)));
  };
  return { x: axis(width, box.x, box.w, ROOM_W), y: axis(height, box.y, box.h, ROOM_H) };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `RoomView`**

Create `mobile/src/components/room/RoomView.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo } from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import { Canvas, Group, Picture, Skia, createPicture, type SkPicture } from '@shopify/react-native-skia';
import { Text } from '../ui/text';
import { drawKipLayer, drawRoomFrames, drawRoomStatic, kipLayerKey, roomPictureKey, type KipSeat } from '../../lib/room/draw';
import { FOCUS_BOX, THUMB_BOX, type RoomRect } from '../../lib/room/grid';
import type { MonitorScreen } from '../../lib/room/items';
import type { KipWorn, RoomLoadout } from '../../lib/room/loadout';
import type { RoomWeather } from '../../lib/room/weather';
import type { KipColour, KipMood, KipPose } from '../../lib/kip/kipArt';
import { useFrameClock } from '../../lib/useFrameClock';
import { useScreenFocused } from '../../lib/useScreenFocused';
import { roomCrop } from './roomCrop';

export const LOOP_MS = 400;

export interface RoomViewProps {
  loadout: RoomLoadout;
  weather: RoomWeather;
  night: boolean;
  colour: KipColour;
  worn: KipWorn;
  pose: KipPose;
  mood: KipMood;
  facing?: 'desk' | 'you';
  screen: MonitorScreen;
  crop: { width: number; height: number };
  cellPt: 1 | 2;
  box?: 'focus' | 'thumb';
  bubble: string | null;
  reduceMotion: boolean;
  accessibilityLabel?: string;
  testID?: string;
}

/** One Path per colour per layer, on whole points, antialiasing off (spec §5). */
function record(layers: readonly (readonly RoomRect[])[], cellPt: number): SkPicture {
  return createPicture((canvas) => {
    for (const rects of layers) {
      const byColour = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof Skia.Path.Make>>();
      for (const [x, y, w, h, colour] of rects) {
        let path = byColour.get(colour);
        if (!path) { path = Skia.Path.Make(); byColour.set(colour, path); }
        path.addRect(Skia.XYWHRect(x * cellPt, y * cellPt, w * cellPt, h * cellPt));
      }
      for (const [colour, path] of byColour) {
        const paint = Skia.Paint();
        paint.setAntiAlias(false);
        paint.setColor(Skia.Color(colour));
        canvas.drawPath(path, paint);
      }
    }
  });
}

/** Releases a picture when it is replaced or the view unmounts (one picture set per screen). */
function useDisposed<T extends { dispose?: () => void }>(value: T): T {
  useEffect(() => () => value.dispose?.(), [value]);
  return value;
}

/** Where the speech bubble's bottom centre sits, in cells: just above KIP's head. */
export function bubbleAnchor(seat: KipSeat): { x: number; y: number } {
  return { x: seat.x + 16, y: seat.y + 10 };
}

// KIP's room (spec §3.2, §5). The static room is recorded once per (loadout, weather, night, colour) into two
// pictures, back and front. KIP and the loop frame are drawn between and over them, so a pose change or a frame
// never rebuilds the room. Loops run at 400 ms and stop when the tab is not focused or under Reduce Motion.
export function RoomView(props: RoomViewProps) {
  const { loadout, weather, night, colour, worn, pose, mood, facing, screen, crop, cellPt, bubble, reduceMotion } = props;
  const focused = useScreenFocused();
  const still = reduceMotion || !focused;
  const t = useFrameClock(still);
  const frame = still ? 0 : Math.floor(t / LOOP_MS) % 3;
  const box = props.box === 'thumb' ? THUMB_BOX : FOCUS_BOX;

  const staticKey = roomPictureKey({ loadout, weather, night, colour });
  const room = useMemo(() => drawRoomStatic({ loadout, weather, night, colour }), [staticKey]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  const back = useDisposed(useMemo(() => record([room.base], cellPt), [room, cellPt]));
  const front = useDisposed(useMemo(() => record([room.light, room.emissive], cellPt), [room, cellPt]));

  const frameKey = `${staticKey}#${screen}#${cellPt}`;
  const framePictures = useMemo(
    () => drawRoomFrames({ loadout, weather, night, colour, screen }).map((f) => record([f], cellPt)),
    [frameKey], // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  );
  useEffect(() => () => framePictures.forEach((p) => p.dispose?.()), [framePictures]);

  const kip = { colour, pose, mood, worn, frame, ...(facing ? { facing } : {}) };
  const kipKey = `${kipLayerKey(kip)}#${room.kipSeat.x},${room.kipSeat.y}#${cellPt}`;
  const kipPicture = useDisposed(useMemo(() => record([drawKipLayer(kip, room.kipSeat)], cellPt), [kipKey])); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  const offset = roomCrop({ width: crop.width, height: crop.height, cellPt, box });
  const anchor = bubbleAnchor(room.kipSeat);
  return (
    <View
      testID={props.testID}
      accessible
      accessibilityRole="image"
      accessibilityLabel={props.accessibilityLabel}
      style={{ width: crop.width, height: crop.height, overflow: 'hidden' }}
    >
      <Canvas style={{ width: crop.width, height: crop.height }}>
        <Group transform={[{ translateX: offset.x }, { translateY: offset.y }]}>
          <Picture picture={back} />
          <Picture picture={kipPicture} />
          <Picture picture={front} />
          <Picture picture={framePictures[frame]!} />
        </Group>
      </Canvas>
      {bubble ? (
        <View
          testID="room-bubble"
          pointerEvents="none"
          accessibilityElementsHidden
          importantForAccessibility="no-hide-descendants"
          className="absolute rounded-lg bg-[#F5F5F4] px-2 py-1"
          style={{ left: Math.max(4, offset.x + anchor.x * cellPt - 36), bottom: crop.height - (offset.y + anchor.y * cellPt) }}
        >
          <Text className="font-pixel text-label text-[#0A0B0E]" numberOfLines={1}>
            {bubble}
          </Text>
        </View>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}
```

`text-[#0A0B0E]` and `bg-[#F5F5F4]` are colour utilities, not font sizes, so the typography guard allows them. The
bubble's ink and paper are fixed light-on-dark colours from W7's `.say`, the same in both themes.

```tsx
import React from 'react';

const WINDOW = { weather: 'cloudy' as const, night: false };

function card(recovery = { score: 58, band: 'scoreFair' as const }, onOpenRecovery = jest.fn()) {
  const s = render(<RoomCard stage={STAGE} window={WINDOW} recovery={recovery} reduceMotion={false} onOpenRecovery={onOpenRecovery} />);
  fireEvent(s.getByTestId('kip-room-frame-inner'), 'layout', { nativeEvent: { layout: { width: 323, height: 200, x: 0, y: 0 } } });
  return s;
}

it('draws the diorama only once the frame has a width, with the stage\'s pose, mood, bubble and screen', () => {
  const s = card();
  expect(s.getByTestId('kip-room').props.room).toBe('type:glance:ON IT…:dots:cloudy:day:coral:2:moving:');
  expect(s.getByTestId('kip-room').props.accessibilityLabel).toBe('KIP at the desk, typing. Window: cloudy');
});

it('captions the card and opens Recovery from the hot tag, with its spoken label', () => {
  const open = jest.fn();
  const s = card(undefined, open);
  expect(s.getByText("KIP'S DESK")).toBeTruthy();
  expect(s.getByText("Window shows today's Recovery")).toBeTruthy();
  const tag = s.getByTestId('kip-tag-recovery');
  expect(tag.props.accessibilityLabel).toBe('Recovery 58, cloudy day. Open Recovery');
  fireEvent.press(tag);
  expect(open).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
});

it('says there is no reading yet on the hot tag without a score', () => {
  const s = card({ score: null, band: null } as never);
  expect(s.getByTestId('kip-tag-recovery').props.accessibilityLabel).toBe('Recovery, no reading yet. Open Recovery');
});
```

```tsx
  it('unavailable: KIP rests, no bubble, "KIP isn\'t available right now."', async () => {
    (fetchCoachStatus as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ ...status, enabled: false });
    const s = render(<CoachScreen />);
    await s.findByText("KIP isn't available right now.");
    expect(room(s)).toMatch(/^rest:sleepy:none:/);
  });

  it('loading: the frame shows a skeleton', async () => {
    (fetchCoachStatus as jest.Mock).mockReturnValue(new Promise(() => {}));
    const s = render(<CoachScreen />);
    expect(s.getByTestId('kip-room-skeleton')).toBeTruthy();
    expect(s.getByTestId('coach-loading')).toBeTruthy();
  });

  it('never opens a picker, even when an old server says no persona was chosen', async () => {
    (fetchCoachStatus as jest.Mock).mockResolvedValue({ ...status, personaChosen: false });
    await openChat();
    expect(mockNavigate).not.toHaveBeenCalledWith('MeetYourCoach', expect.anything());
  });

  it('the prompt bar asks KIP', async () => {
    const s = await openChat();
    expect(s.getByTestId('coach-input').props.placeholder).toBe('Ask KIP…');
    expect(s.getByTestId('coach-input').props.accessibilityLabel).toBe('Message KIP');
  });
});
```

Create `mobile/src/lib/kipStage.ts`:

```ts
// KIP's states on the KIP page (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §3.3, §3.4) as pure functions: the room's pose, mood,
// bubble and monitor; the status line; and the spoken labels. CoachScreen derives the input; the room, the header
// and the collapsed header read the result.
import type { KipMood, KipPose } from './kip/kipArt';
import { KIP_COPY, verdictWord } from './kipCopy';
import type { MonitorScreen } from './room/items';
import type { RoomWeather } from './room/weather';
import type { ScoreBand } from './scoreInsights';

export type KipStageId = 'loading' | 'empty' | 'idle' | 'thinking' | 'answering' | 'error' | 'offline' | 'unavailable' | 'consent';

export interface KipStage {
  id: KipStageId;
  pose: KipPose;
  mood: KipMood;
  bubble: string | null;
  screen: MonitorScreen;
}

export interface KipStageInput {
  phase: 'loading' | 'unavailable' | 'needs-consent' | 'ready';
  statusUnverified: boolean;
  offline: boolean;
  /** Waiting for the first sentence. */
  waiting: boolean;
  /** A turn is running (waiting or sentences arriving). */
  streaming: boolean;
  /** The error card is showing. */
  error: boolean;
  /** No messages yet. */
  empty: boolean;
  /** The day's pose and mood: Home F's sceneState mapping (useTodayScene). */
  idle: { pose: KipPose; mood: KipMood };
}

const at = (id: KipStageId, pose: KipPose, mood: KipMood, bubble: string | null = null, screen: MonitorScreen = 'lines'): KipStage =>
  ({ id, pose, mood, bubble, screen });

export function kipStage(i: KipStageInput): KipStage {
  if (i.phase === 'loading') return at('loading', 'rest', 'idle');
  if (i.phase === 'unavailable') return at('unavailable', 'rest', 'sleepy');
  if (i.phase === 'needs-consent') return at('consent', 'wave', 'curious', 'MAY I?');
  if (i.waiting) return at('thinking', 'type', 'glance', 'ON IT…', 'dots');
  if (i.streaming) return at('answering', 'point', 'glance', "HERE'S MY TAKE", 'chart');
  if (i.offline) return at('offline', 'rest', 'sleepy', 'Z Z');
  if (i.error) return at('error', 'shrug', 'startled', 'OOPS');
  if (i.statusUnverified) return at('unavailable', 'rest', 'sleepy');
  if (i.empty) return at('empty', 'wave', 'idle', 'HI!');
  return at('idle', i.idle.pose, i.idle.mood);
}

const POCKET = 'In the pocket room';

export function kipStatusLine(stage: KipStageId, recovery: { score: number | null; band: ScoreBand | null }, collapsed: boolean): string {
  if (stage === 'thinking') return 'Typing it up…';
  if (stage === 'offline') return 'Offline';
  if (stage === 'answering') return collapsed && recovery.score !== null ? `Answering · Recovery ${recovery.score}` : 'Answering';
  const verdict = recovery.score === null ? null : verdictWord(recovery.band);
  return !collapsed && verdict && (stage === 'idle' || stage === 'empty') ? `${POCKET} · ${verdict} day` : POCKET;
}

const DOING: Record<KipStageId, string> = {
  loading: 'resting', empty: 'waving', idle: 'resting', thinking: 'typing', answering: 'turned to face you',
  error: 'shrugging', offline: 'asleep', unavailable: 'asleep', consent: 'waving',
};
const SKY_WORD: Record<RoomWeather, string> = {
  clear: 'clear', mostly: 'mostly clear', cloudy: 'cloudy', stormy: 'stormy', short: 'hazy', fallback: 'mostly clear',
};

export function windowWord(w: { weather: RoomWeather; night: boolean }): string {
  return w.night ? 'night' : SKY_WORD[w.weather];
}

/** The diorama's one label (§3.2): "KIP at the desk, typing. Window: cloudy". */
export function roomLabel(stage: KipStageId, window: { weather: RoomWeather; night: boolean }): string {
  return `KIP at the desk, ${DOING[stage]}. Window: ${windowWord(window)}`;
}

/** The hot tag's spoken label (§3.1). */
export function hotTagLabel(score: number | null, window: { weather: RoomWeather; night: boolean }): string {
  return score === null ? 'Recovery, no reading yet. Open Recovery' : `Recovery ${score}, ${windowWord(window)} day. Open Recovery`;
}
```

`KIP_COPY` is imported here only for the doc trail. Remove the import if it is unused, so lint stays clean.

Create `mobile/src/components/home/HotTag.tsx`. Move `HotTag` from `HomeScene.tsx:70-94` and make its position optional:

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { Pressable, View } from 'react-native';
import { clampTagLeft } from '../../lib/home/scene';
import { Text } from '../ui/text';

const INK = '#F5F5F4';

// Home F's hot tag (dark, rounded-lg, a band-colour square, a pixel key, a tabular value). Home places it over the
// scene (`position`); the KIP page's room card lays it out in normal flow (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §3.1).
export function HotTag({ testID, keyText, keyColour, value, label, onPress, position }: {
  testID: string; keyText: string; keyColour: string; value: string | null; label: string; onPress: () => void;
  position?: { left: number; top: number; screenWidth: number };
}) {
  const [width, setWidth] = useState(0);
  const placed = position
    ? { position: 'absolute' as const, left: clampTagLeft(position.left, width, position.screenWidth), top: position.top }
    : { alignSelf: 'flex-start' as const };
  return (
    <Pressable
      testID={testID}
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityLabel={label}
      onPress={onPress}
      hitSlop={8}
      onLayout={(e) => setWidth(e.nativeEvent.layout.width)}
      className="flex-row items-center rounded-lg border active:opacity-80"
      style={{ ...placed, height: 26, gap: 5, paddingHorizontal: 7, backgroundColor: 'rgba(10,11,14,0.82)', borderColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.18)' }}
    >
      <View style={{ width: 4, height: 4, backgroundColor: keyColour }} />
      <Text className="font-pixel text-label" style={{ color: keyColour }}>{keyText}</Text>
      {value !== null ? <Text className="text-caption font-semibold tabular-nums" style={{ color: INK }}>{value}</Text> : null}
    </Pressable>
  );
}
```

Create `mobile/src/components/coach/RoomCard.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useState } from 'react';
import { View, type ViewProps } from 'react-native';
import { Card } from '../ui/card';
import { SectionLabel } from '../ui/section-label';
import { Skeleton } from '../ui/skeleton';
import { Text } from '../ui/text';
import { HotTag } from '../home/HotTag';
import { RoomView } from '../room/RoomView';
import { HOME_COPY } from '../../lib/homeCopy';
import { useMyKipColour } from '../../lib/kip/myKipColour';
import { KIP_COPY } from '../../lib/kipCopy';
import { hotTagLabel, roomLabel, type KipStage } from '../../lib/kipStage';
import { EMPTY_WORN, STARTER_LOADOUT } from '../../lib/room/loadout';
import type { RoomWeather } from '../../lib/room/weather';
import type { ScoreBand } from '../../lib/scoreInsights';
import { COLORS } from '../../theme';

export const ROOM_FRAME_HEIGHT = 200;
const WOOD = '#5B4636';
const WOOD_LINE = '#3A2D23';

// The W7 room card (spec §3.1, §3.2): KIP's room as a framed diorama (crop, never shrink: the inner width is
// measured, the height fixed at 200), a caption row with the RECOVERY hot tag, and an optional footer (Task 10's
// "Shrink room"). The frame is part of the picture, not a button.
export function RoomCard({ stage, window, recovery, reduceMotion, onOpenRecovery, footer, onLayout }: {
  stage: KipStage;
  window: { weather: RoomWeather; night: boolean };
  recovery: { score: number | null; band: ScoreBand | null };
  reduceMotion: boolean;
  onOpenRecovery: () => void;
  footer?: React.ReactNode;
  onLayout?: ViewProps['onLayout'];
}) {
  const colour = useMyKipColour();
  const [inner, setInner] = useState(0);
  return (
    <Card testID="kip-room-card" className="gap-3 p-3.5" onLayout={onLayout}>
      <View testID="kip-room-frame" style={{ borderWidth: 2, borderColor: WOOD_LINE, borderRadius: 4 }}>
        <View style={{ borderWidth: 6, borderColor: WOOD, borderRadius: 2 }}>
          <View
            testID="kip-room-frame-inner"
            style={{ height: ROOM_FRAME_HEIGHT, overflow: 'hidden', backgroundColor: '#0A0B0E' }}
            onLayout={(e) => setInner(Math.floor(e.nativeEvent.layout.width))}
          >
            {stage.id === 'loading' ? (
              <Skeleton testID="kip-room-skeleton" className="h-full w-full rounded-none" />
            ) : inner > 0 ? (
              <RoomView
                testID="kip-room"
                loadout={STARTER_LOADOUT}
                worn={EMPTY_WORN}
                weather={window.weather}
                night={window.night}
                colour={colour}
                pose={stage.pose}
                mood={stage.mood}
                screen={stage.screen}
                bubble={stage.bubble}
                crop={{ width: inner, height: ROOM_FRAME_HEIGHT }}
                cellPt={2}
                reduceMotion={reduceMotion}
                accessibilityLabel={roomLabel(stage.id, window)}
              />
            ) : null}
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>
      <View className="flex-row flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">
        <View className="shrink gap-0.5">
          <SectionLabel>{KIP_COPY.deskLabel}</SectionLabel>
          <Text className="text-caption text-muted-foreground">{KIP_COPY.windowCaption}</Text>
        </View>
        <HotTag
          testID="kip-tag-recovery"
          keyText={KIP_COPY.recoveryTag}
          keyColour={recovery.band ? COLORS.dark[recovery.band] : COLORS.dark.muted}
          value={recovery.score === null ? HOME_COPY.none : String(recovery.score)}
          label={hotTagLabel(recovery.score, window)}
          onPress={onOpenRecovery}
        />
      </View>
      {footer}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`SectionLabel` is light by default; if it takes a `tone` prop, pass the light one (W7: "KIP'S DESK" light). Check
`components/ui/section-label.tsx`. `Skeleton` accepts `testID` through its view props; if it does not, wrap it in a
`View testID="kip-room-skeleton"`.

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import { Button } from '../ui/button';
import { Text } from '../ui/text';
import { KIP_COPY } from '../../lib/kipCopy';

// The thread's card for the states with no chat (spec §3.4): consent (two buttons), offline and unavailable (no
// button: there is no outbox and no offline cache of past chats).
export function KipStateCard({ kind, onReview, onNotNow }: { kind: 'consent' | 'offline' | 'unavailable'; onReview?: () => void; onNotNow?: () => void }) {
  if (kind === 'consent') {
    return (
      <View testID="kip-consent" className="gap-3 rounded-tile border border-border bg-card p-4">
        <Text accessibilityRole="header" className="text-heading">{KIP_COPY.consentHeading}</Text>
        <Text className="text-body text-muted-foreground">{KIP_COPY.consentBody}</Text>
        <View className="flex-row gap-2">
          <Button testID="coach-review-consent-button" className="flex-1" onPress={onReview}>{KIP_COPY.consentReview}</Button>
          <Button testID="kip-consent-not-now" variant="outline" className="flex-1" onPress={onNotNow}>{KIP_COPY.consentNotNow}</Button>
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }
  return (
    <View testID={`kip-${kind}`} accessibilityRole={kind === 'offline' ? 'alert' : undefined} className="rounded-tile border border-dashed border-border px-3.5 py-3">
      <Text className="text-body text-muted-foreground">{kind === 'offline' ? KIP_COPY.offline : KIP_COPY.unavailable}</Text>
    </View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
  // A send that never left the device (useCoachConversation's 'offline' kind): show offline and put the question
  // back in the bar, unless something new is already typed there.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (error?.kind !== 'offline') return;
    setOffline(true);
    setInput((current) => current || error.request.message);
  }, [error, setOffline]);

  // The next request that gets through clears offline.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (answeredAt !== null || conversationId) setOffline(false);
  }, [answeredAt, conversationId, setOffline]);

  // Back to the foreground while offline: try the status again.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!offline) return undefined;
    const sub = AppState.addEventListener('change', (state) => { if (state === 'active') void loadRef.current(); });
    return () => sub.remove();
  }, [offline]);
```

Create `mobile/__tests__/lib/kipRoomPrefs.test.ts`:

```ts
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';
import { readKipRoomShrunk, writeKipRoomShrunk } from '../../src/lib/kipRoomPrefs';

jest.mock('expo-secure-store', () => ({ getItemAsync: jest.fn(), setItemAsync: jest.fn() }));
const store = SecureStore as jest.Mocked<typeof SecureStore>;
beforeEach(() => jest.resetAllMocks());

it('defaults to expanded, and reads "1" as shrunk', async () => {
  store.getItemAsync.mockResolvedValueOnce(null);
  expect(await readKipRoomShrunk()).toBe(false);
  store.getItemAsync.mockResolvedValueOnce('1');
  expect(await readKipRoomShrunk()).toBe(true);
});

it('a keychain failure reads as expanded and a failed write is swallowed', async () => {
  store.getItemAsync.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('locked'));
  expect(await readKipRoomShrunk()).toBe(false);
  store.setItemAsync.mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error('locked'));
  await expect(writeKipRoomShrunk(true)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
});

it('writes under kipRoomShrunk', async () => {
  await writeKipRoomShrunk(true);
  expect(store.setItemAsync).toHaveBeenCalledWith('kipRoomShrunk', '1');
  await writeKipRoomShrunk(false);
  expect(store.setItemAsync).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('kipRoomShrunk', '0');
});
```

Create `mobile/__tests__/lib/useKipTurn.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { act, renderHook } from '@testing-library/react-native';
import { TURN_FRAME_MS, useKipTurn } from '../../src/lib/useKipTurn';
import type { KipStage } from '../../src/lib/kipStage';

const THINKING: KipStage = { id: 'thinking', pose: 'type', mood: 'glance', bubble: 'ON IT…', screen: 'dots' };
const ANSWERING: KipStage = { id: 'answering', pose: 'point', mood: 'glance', bubble: "HERE'S MY TAKE", screen: 'chart' };
const IDLE: KipStage = { id: 'idle', pose: 'rest', mood: 'idle', bubble: null, screen: 'lines' };

beforeEach(() => jest.useFakeTimers());
afterEach(() => jest.useRealTimers());

it('types facing the desk', () => {
  const { result } = renderHook(() => useKipTurn(THINKING, false));
  expect(result.current).toEqual({ pose: 'type', mood: 'glance', facing: 'desk' });
});

it('turns from the desk to you over two 80 ms frames, then points (§4)', () => {
  const { result, rerender } = renderHook(({ s }) => useKipTurn(s, false), { initialProps: { s: THINKING } });
  rerender({ s: ANSWERING });
  expect(result.current).toEqual({ pose: 'rest', mood: 'glance', facing: 'desk' });
  act(() => { jest.advanceTimersByTime(TURN_FRAME_MS); });
  expect(result.current).toEqual({ pose: 'rest', mood: 'glance', facing: 'you' });
  act(() => { jest.advanceTimersByTime(TURN_FRAME_MS); });
  expect(result.current).toEqual({ pose: 'point', mood: 'glance', facing: 'you' });
});

it('cuts straight to point under Reduce Motion', () => {
  const { result, rerender } = renderHook(({ s }) => useKipTurn(s, true), { initialProps: { s: THINKING } });
  rerender({ s: ANSWERING });
  expect(result.current).toEqual({ pose: 'point', mood: 'glance', facing: 'you' });
  expect(jest.getTimerCount()).toBe(0);
});

it('any other change is immediate', () => {
  const { result, rerender } = renderHook(({ s }) => useKipTurn(s, false), { initialProps: { s: ANSWERING } });
  rerender({ s: IDLE });
  expect(result.current).toEqual({ pose: 'rest', mood: 'idle', facing: 'you' });
});
```

```tsx
let mockReduceMotion = false;
jest.spyOn(AccessibilityInfo, 'isReduceMotionEnabled').mockImplementation(() => Promise.resolve(mockReduceMotion));

const prefs = jest.requireMock('../../src/lib/kipRoomPrefs');
const scrollTo = (s: ReturnType<typeof render>, y: number) =>
  fireEvent.scroll(s.getByTestId('coach-scroll'), { nativeEvent: { contentOffset: { x: 0, y }, contentSize: { width: 375, height: 2000 }, layoutMeasurement: { width: 375, height: 700 } } });
const layCard = (s: ReturnType<typeof render>) =>
  fireEvent(s.getByTestId('kip-room-card'), 'layout', { nativeEvent: { layout: { x: 20, y: 12, width: 335, height: 300 } } });

beforeEach(() => {
  mockReduceMotion = false;
  prefs.readKipRoomShrunk.mockResolvedValue(false);
});

it('collapse: the header appears once the card\'s bottom (312) passes under it (60), fading over 24 points', async () => {
  const s = await openChat();
  layCard(s);
  scrollTo(s, 200);
  expect(s.queryByTestId('kip-collapsed-header')).toBeNull();
  scrollTo(s, 240);
  expect(s.getByTestId('kip-collapsed-header').props.style).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({ opacity: 0.5 }));
  scrollTo(s, 260);
  expect(s.getByTestId('kip-collapsed-header')).toBeTruthy();
  expect(s.getByTestId('kip-collapsed-status')).toHaveTextContent('In the pocket room');
});

it('under Reduce Motion the collapsed header switches at the threshold, with no fade', async () => {
  mockReduceMotion = true;
  const s = await openChat();
  layCard(s);
  scrollTo(s, 240);
  expect(s.queryByTestId('kip-collapsed-header')).toBeNull();
  scrollTo(s, 252);
  expect(s.getByTestId('kip-collapsed-header').props.style).toEqual(expect.objectContaining({ opacity: 1 }));
});

it('the thumbnail scrolls back to the top', async () => {
  const s = await openChat();
  layCard(s);
  scrollTo(s, 400);
  const spy = jest.spyOn(ScrollView.prototype, 'scrollTo');
  fireEvent.press(s.getByTestId('kip-room-thumbnail'));
  expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ y: 0, animated: true });
  expect(s.getByTestId('kip-room-thumbnail').props.accessibilityLabel).toBe("Expand KIP's room");
});

it('"Shrink room" collapses for good, persists, and survives a remount', async () => {
  const s = await openChat();
  fireEvent.press(s.getByTestId('kip-shrink-room'));
  expect(prefs.writeKipRoomShrunk).toHaveBeenCalledWith(true);
  expect(s.queryByTestId('kip-room-card')).toBeNull();
  expect(s.getByTestId('kip-collapsed-header')).toBeTruthy();
  s.unmount();
  prefs.readKipRoomShrunk.mockResolvedValue(true);
  const again = await openChat();
  await waitFor(() => expect(again.queryByTestId('kip-room-card')).toBeNull());
  fireEvent.press(again.getByTestId('kip-room-thumbnail'));
  expect(prefs.writeKipRoomShrunk).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(false);
  expect(again.getByTestId('kip-room-card')).toBeTruthy();
});

it('a storage failure leaves the card expanded', async () => {
  prefs.readKipRoomShrunk.mockResolvedValue(false); // readKipRoomShrunk swallows the failure itself (kipRoomPrefs.test)
  const s = await openChat();
  expect(s.getByTestId('kip-room-card')).toBeTruthy();
});

it('the prefill still lands in the input with the room on screen, and survives the consent round-trip', async () => {
  mockParams = { prefill: 'Why is my recovery where it is today?' };
  const s = await openChat();
  expect(s.getByTestId('coach-input').props.value).toBe('Why is my recovery where it is today?');
});

it('passes Reduce Motion to the room (loops off)', async () => {
  mockReduceMotion = true;
  const s = await openChat();
  fireEvent(s.getByTestId('kip-room-frame-inner'), 'layout', { nativeEvent: { layout: { width: 323, height: 200, x: 0, y: 0 } } });
  await waitFor(() => expect(s.getByTestId('kip-room').props.room).toMatch(/:still:/));
});
```

The opacity numbers: the card's bottom is 12 + 300 = 312. The header is 60 tall, so it is fully in at scroll
312 − 60 = 252 and starts at 228. At 240, progress is 12 / 24 = 0.5.

Create `mobile/src/lib/kipRoomPrefs.ts`:

```ts
import * as SecureStore from 'expo-secure-store';

// "Shrink room" (spec §3.5): remembered on this device only, never sent to the server. The repo's device-storage
// pattern (as the thinking cache): a keychain failure reads as the default, expanded.
const KEY = 'kipRoomShrunk';

export async function readKipRoomShrunk(): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    return (await SecureStore.getItemAsync(KEY)) === '1';
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

export async function writeKipRoomShrunk(shrunk: boolean): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await SecureStore.setItemAsync(KEY, shrunk ? '1' : '0');
  } catch {
    // The choice still holds for this session.
  }
}
```

Create `mobile/src/lib/useKipTurn.ts`:

```ts
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import type { KipMood, KipPose } from './kip/kipArt';
import type { KipStage } from './kipStage';

export const TURN_FRAME_MS = 80;

type Look = { pose: KipPose; mood: KipMood; facing: 'desk' | 'you' };
const lookOf = (s: KipStage): Look => ({ pose: s.pose, mood: s.mood, facing: s.pose === 'type' ? 'desk' : 'you' });
// Plan ruling 7: the turn is two 80 ms frames (still glancing, facing the desk, then you) before pointing.
const TURN: readonly Look[] = [
  { pose: 'rest', mood: 'glance', facing: 'desk' },
  { pose: 'rest', mood: 'glance', facing: 'you' },
];

// KIP turns to face you when the first sentence arrives (spec §4): a 2-frame swap over 160 ms. Every other change
// is immediate; Reduce Motion cuts.
export function useKipTurn(stage: KipStage, reduceMotion: boolean): Look {
  const previous = useRef(stage.id);
  const [frame, setFrame] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const turning = previous.current === 'thinking' && stage.id === 'answering' && !reduceMotion;
  if (previous.current !== stage.id) {
    previous.current = stage.id;
    if (turning && frame === null) setFrame(0);
    else if (!turning && frame !== null) setFrame(null);
  }
  useEffect(() => {
    if (frame === null) return undefined;
    const id = setTimeout(() => setFrame((f) => (f !== null && f + 1 < TURN.length ? f + 1 : null)), TURN_FRAME_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(id);
  }, [frame]);
  return frame === null ? lookOf(stage) : TURN[frame]!;
}
```

Setting state during render (the `if (previous.current !== stage.id)` block) is React's sanctioned "adjust state when
a prop changes" pattern. It makes the first turn frame show on the same render as the stage change, which the test
checks. If the implementer prefers an effect, the test needs a flush before the first expectation.

Create `mobile/src/components/coach/CollapsedKipHeader.tsx`:

```tsx
import React from 'react';
import { Pressable, View } from 'react-native';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { useColorScheme } from 'nativewind';
import { Button, buttonIconSize } from '../ui/button';
import { PageTitle } from '../ui/page-title';
import { Text } from '../ui/text';
import { RoomView } from '../room/RoomView';
import { useMyKipColour } from '../../lib/kip/myKipColour';
import { KIP_COPY } from '../../lib/kipCopy';
import type { KipStage } from '../../lib/kipStage';
import { EMPTY_WORN, STARTER_LOADOUT } from '../../lib/room/loadout';
import type { RoomWeather } from '../../lib/room/weather';
import { COLORS } from '../../theme';

export const COLLAPSED_HEIGHT = 60;
export const FADE_PT = 24;

/** 0 → 1 over the last 24 points before the card's bottom edge passes under the 60-point header (§3.5). */
export function collapseProgress(scrollY: number, cardBottom: number): number {
  const end = cardBottom - COLLAPSED_HEIGHT;
  return Math.max(0, Math.min(1, (scrollY - (end - FADE_PT)) / FADE_PT));
}

// W7 "Collapsed · after scroll" (spec §3.5): the room's thumbnail (96 × 60 at 1 point a cell, cropped to THUMB_BOX,
// inside a 3-point frame), "KIP" with the collapsed status line, and the Conversations and New chat buttons.
export function CollapsedKipHeader({ stage, look, statusLine, window, reduceMotion, onExpand, onConversations, onNewChat, showActions }: {
  stage: KipStage;
  look: { pose: KipStage['pose']; mood: KipStage['mood']; facing: 'desk' | 'you' };
  statusLine: string;
  window: { weather: RoomWeather; night: boolean };
  reduceMotion: boolean;
  onExpand: () => void;
  onConversations: () => void;
  onNewChat: () => void;
  showActions: boolean;
}) {
  const colour = useMyKipColour();
  const { colorScheme } = useColorScheme();
  const colors = colorScheme === 'dark' ? COLORS.dark : COLORS.light;
  return (
    <View className="flex-row items-center gap-3 px-5" style={{ height: COLLAPSED_HEIGHT + 12 }}>
      <Pressable
        testID="kip-room-thumbnail"
        accessibilityRole="button"
        accessibilityLabel={KIP_COPY.expandRoomLabel}
        onPress={onExpand}
        style={{ borderWidth: 3, borderColor: '#5B4636', borderRadius: 4 }}
      >
        <RoomView
          testID="kip-room-thumb"
          loadout={STARTER_LOADOUT}
          worn={EMPTY_WORN}
          weather={window.weather}
          night={window.night}
          colour={colour}
          pose={look.pose}
          mood={look.mood}
          facing={look.facing}
          screen={stage.screen}
          bubble={null}
          crop={{ width: 96, height: 60 }}
          cellPt={1}
          box="thumb"
          reduceMotion={reduceMotion}
        />
      </Pressable>
      <View className="shrink grow gap-0.5">
        <PageTitle>{KIP_COPY.pageTitle}</PageTitle>
        <Text testID="kip-collapsed-status" numberOfLines={1} className="text-caption text-muted-foreground">{statusLine}</Text>
      </View>
      {showActions ? (
        <View className="flex-row gap-2">
          <Button testID="kip-collapsed-conversations" variant="outline" size="icon-lg" accessibilityLabel={KIP_COPY.conversationsLabel} onPress={onConversations}>
            <Ionicons name="menu-outline" size={buttonIconSize('icon-lg')} color={colors.foreground} />
          </Button>
          <Button testID="kip-collapsed-new-chat" variant="outline" size="icon-lg" accessibilityLabel={KIP_COPY.newChatLabel} onPress={onNewChat}>
            <Ionicons name="create-outline" size={buttonIconSize('icon-lg')} color={colors.foreground} />
          </Button>
        </View>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}
```

The thumbnail's `RoomView` is decorative here: the pressable carries the label, and RoomView gets no
`accessibilityLabel`. Pass `accessible={false}` through if RoomView exposes it. If not, add an optional `accessible`
prop to `RoomView`, defaulting to `true`.

Add `shrinkRoom: 'Shrink room'` and `expandRoomLabel: "Expand KIP's room"` to `KIP_COPY`.

In `CoachScreen.tsx`:
1. **Reduce Motion from `AccessibilityInfo`** (spec §3.6), replacing Task 9's `useReducedMotion()`:

```tsx
  const [reduceMotion, setReduceMotion] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    void AccessibilityInfo.isReduceMotionEnabled().then((v) => { if (live) setReduceMotion(v); });
    const sub = AccessibilityInfo.addEventListener('reduceMotionChanged', setReduceMotion);
    return () => { live = false; sub.remove(); };
  }, []);
```

2. **Shrunk:**

```tsx
  const [shrunk, setShrunk] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    let live = true;
    void readKipRoomShrunk().then((v) => { if (live) setShrunk(v); });
    return () => { live = false; };
  }, []);
  const shrink = useCallback(() => { setShrunk(true); void writeKipRoomShrunk(true); }, []);
  const expand = useCallback(() => {
    if (shrunk) { setShrunk(false); void writeKipRoomShrunk(false); }
    scrollRef.current?.scrollTo({ y: 0, animated: true });
  }, [shrunk]);
```

3. **Collapse tracking:** `const [cardBottom, setCardBottom] = useState<number | null>(null);`, `const [progress,
   setProgress] = useState(0);`. The room card gets
   `onLayout={(e) => setCardBottom(e.nativeEvent.layout.y + e.nativeEvent.layout.height)}`. The `ScrollView` gets
   `scrollEventThrottle={16}` and:

```tsx
            onScroll={(e) => {
              if (cardBottom === null) return;
              const p = collapseProgress(e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y, cardBottom);
              setProgress((prev) => (prev === p ? prev : p));
            }}
```

   This JS handler only changes state when the rounded progress changes. The value is an exact multiple of 1/24
   between the bounds and 0 or 1 outside them, so a long scroll past the card sets state once.
4. **The look and the room:** `const look = useKipTurn(stage, reduceMotion);`. `RoomCard` receives `look` so the
   diorama draws `look.pose`, `look.mood` and `look.facing`. Add a `look` prop to `RoomCard` and pass it to
   `RoomView`; the stage still supplies the bubble and the screen. Pass the footer:

```tsx
footer={<Button testID="kip-shrink-room" variant="outline" className="w-full" onPress={shrink}>{KIP_COPY.shrinkRoom}</Button>}
```

5. **Render:**
   - When `shrunk`: no `RoomCard` in the scroll. The page's top shows `<CollapsedKipHeader …/>` in flow in place of the
     expanded `header` (opacity 1). `statusLine` is `kipStatusLine(stage.id, day.recovery, true)`.
   - Otherwise, an absolutely positioned overlay inside the `SafeAreaView`. It is rendered only while `progress > 0`,
     so `queryByTestId` is null before the fade starts:

```tsx
      {!shrunk && progress > 0 ? (
        <View
          testID="kip-collapsed-header"
          className="absolute left-0 right-0 top-0 z-10 bg-background"
          pointerEvents={progress >= 1 ? 'auto' : 'none'}
          style={{ opacity: reduceMotion ? (progress >= 1 ? 1 : 0) : progress }}
        >
          <CollapsedKipHeader … />
        </View>
      ) : null}
```

   Under Reduce Motion, render the overlay only when `progress >= 1`, so it is absent below the threshold (the test
   checks this). The opacity is a plain style here, set from the scroll offset with no timers. If the implementer moves
   it to a Reanimated shared value (`useSharedValue`, set in the same `onScroll`, read by `useAnimatedStyle`), keep the
   `testID` on the `Animated.View` and assert opacity through `jest-native`'s `toHaveAnimatedStyle`.
6. **Keyboard:** the card has a fixed size, so it does not resize when the keyboard opens. Nothing changes in
   `KeyboardAvoidingView`.
7. **Actions:** `showActions` is `ready`. `onConversations` is `() => setSheetOpen(true)`. `onNewChat` is
   `startNewChat`.

Create `mobile/src/components/coach/AskKipBar.tsx`:

```tsx
import React, { useContext } from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaInsetsContext } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useColorScheme } from 'nativewind';
import { Ionicons } from '@expo/vector-icons';
import { COLORS } from '../../theme';
import { GlassSurface } from '../ui/glass-surface';
import { PressableScale } from '../ui/pressable-scale';
import { Text } from '../ui/text';
import { KipHead } from '../kip/KipHead';
import type { KipMood } from '../../lib/kip/kipArt';

// The one documented custom CTA (components/ui/README.md): a GlassSurface bar with KIP's head (scale 4, your colour,
// the page's mood), 8 px corners, pinned above the safe area. Used by Sleep and Recovery (spec §7.2).
export function AskKipBar({ label, mood, onPress }: { label: string; mood: KipMood; onPress: () => void }) {
  const { colorScheme: scheme } = useColorScheme();
  const colors = scheme === 'dark' ? COLORS.dark : COLORS.light;
  // Context, not the hook: tests render screens without a provider.
  const insets = useContext(SafeAreaInsetsContext);
  return (
    <View className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 px-5" style={{ paddingBottom: Math.max(insets?.bottom ?? 0, 16) }} pointerEvents="box-none">
      <PressableScale testID="ask-kip-button" accessibilityRole="button" accessibilityLabel={label} onPress={onPress}>
        <GlassSurface
          scheme={scheme === 'light' ? 'light' : 'dark'}
          fallbackColor={colors.surfaceRaised}
          borderRadius={8}
          style={{ height: 60, flexDirection: 'row', alignItems: 'center', gap: 12, paddingHorizontal: 10, borderWidth: 1, borderColor: colors.hairline }}
        >
          <KipHead testID="ask-kip-head" mood={mood} scale={4} />
          <Text className="flex-1 text-body font-semibold">{label}</Text>
          <Ionicons name="chevron-forward" size={18} color={colors.muted} style={{ marginRight: 8 }} />
        </GlassSurface>
      </PressableScale>
    </View>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { KipSettingsSection } from '../../src/components/kip-settings-section';
import { revokeCoachConsent, listCoachMemory, type CoachStatusDTO } from '../../src/api/coach';

jest.mock('../../src/api/coach', () => ({ ...jest.requireActual('../../src/api/coach'), revokeCoachConsent: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve()), listCoachMemory: jest.fn(() => Promise.resolve([{ id: 'm1' }, { id: 'm2' }])) }));

const ON: CoachStatusDTO = { enabled: true, consented: true, consent: { version: '2', summary: '', dataItems: [] }, personaId: 'kip', personaChosen: true, personas: [], thinkingText: 'steps', engine: 'local' };
const OFF: CoachStatusDTO = { ...ON, consented: false };

beforeEach(() => { mockNavigate.mockClear(); setMyKipColour('cobalt'); });
afterEach(() => resetMyKipColour());

});
```

Create `mobile/src/components/kip-settings-section.tsx` (it merges `coach-settings-section.tsx` and `your-coach-row.tsx`):

```tsx
import React, { useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { View } from 'react-native';
import { listCoachMemory, revokeCoachConsent } from '../api/coach';
import { useCoachStatusContext } from '../lib/coachStatus/CoachStatusProvider';
import { useMyKipColour } from '../lib/kip/myKipColour';
import { kipColourName } from '../lib/kip/kipColour';
import { KIP_COPY } from '../lib/kipCopy';
import { DEFAULT_THINKING_TEXT, THINKING_TEXT_NAMES } from '../lib/thinkingText';
import { useCoachStatus } from '../lib/useCoachStatus';
import { ScreenNavigationContext } from '../lib/useScreenFocused';
import { AiEngineRow } from './ai-engine-row';
import { Kip } from './kip/Kip';
import { Button } from './ui/button';
import { SettingsGroup, SettingsRow } from './ui/settings-list';
import { Text } from './ui/text';

// Settings → KIP (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §7.5): your KIP, how the chat shows KIP working, memory and privacy,
// and turning coaching off. Replaces "Your coach" and "AI Coach". There is no answer-style row (dropped by the owner).
// Context, not useNavigation(): Settings also renders outside a navigator.
export function KipSettingsSection() {
  const navigation = useContext(ScreenNavigationContext);
  const go = (name: string, params?: object) => navigation?.navigate?.(name, params);
  const colour = useMyKipColour();
  const thinking = useCoachStatusContext()?.thinkingText ?? DEFAULT_THINKING_TEXT;
  const { status, setStatus } = useCoachStatus();
  const consented = !!status?.enabled && status.consented;
  const [memoryCount, setMemoryCount] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [revokeError, setRevokeError] = useState(false);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!consented) return undefined;
    let live = true;
    listCoachMemory()
      .then((items) => { if (live) setMemoryCount(items.length); })
      .catch(() => { if (live) setMemoryCount(null); });
    return () => { live = false; };
  }, [consented]);

  async function revoke() {
    if (!status || busy) return;
    setRevokeError(false);
    setBusy(true);
    try {
      await revokeCoachConsent();
      setStatus({ ...status, consented: false });
    } catch {
      setRevokeError(true);
    } finally {
      setBusy(false);
    }
  }

  const footer = !status?.enabled ? undefined : consented ? KIP_COPY.onFooter : KIP_COPY.offFooter;
  return (
    <View testID="kip-settings" className="gap-6">
      <SettingsGroup label={KIP_COPY.settingsGroup} footer={footer}>
        <SettingsRow
          testID="kip-your-kip"
          leading={<Kip colour={colour} pose="wave" mood="idle" scale={2} />}
          title="KIP"
          subtitle={KIP_COPY.yourKipSubtitle(kipColourName(colour))}
          trailing={
            <Button testID="kip-change-colour" variant="outline" size="sm" onPress={() => go('ProfileSection', { section: 'kipColour' })}>
              {KIP_COPY.changeColour}
            </Button>
          }
        />
        <SettingsRow
          testID="kip-thinking-text"
          icon="chatbubble-ellipses-outline"
          title={KIP_COPY.whileKipThinks}
          subtitle={KIP_COPY.whileKipThinksSubtitle}
          value={THINKING_TEXT_NAMES[thinking].name}
          onPress={() => go('ThinkingText')}
        />
        {status?.enabled && !consented ? (
          <SettingsRow testID="coach-setup-button" icon="sparkles-outline" title={KIP_COPY.setUp} onPress={() => go('CoachConsent')} />
        ) : null}
        {consented ? (
          <>
            <SettingsRow
              testID="kip-memory-row"
              icon="bulb-outline"
              title={KIP_COPY.memoryTitle}
              subtitle={memoryCount === null ? undefined : KIP_COPY.memorySubtitle(memoryCount)}
              onPress={() => go('CoachMemory')}
            />
            <SettingsRow testID="kip-what-is-sent" icon="shield-checkmark-outline" title={KIP_COPY.whatIsSent} onPress={() => go('CoachConsent')} />
          </>
        ) : null}
      </SettingsGroup>
      {consented && status ? (
        <>
          <AiEngineRow status={status} onChange={setStatus} onChooseHosted={() => go('HostedConsent')} />
          <Button testID="coach-revoke-button" variant="destructive" className="border border-destructive/40" disabled={busy} onPress={() => void revoke()}>
            {KIP_COPY.turnOff}
          </Button>
          {revokeError ? (
            <Text testID="coach-revoke-error" className="-mt-4 px-4 text-caption text-destructive">{KIP_COPY.turnOffError}</Text>
          ) : null}
        </>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}
```

`ScreenNavigation.navigate` is typed `(name: string, params?: object) => void` (`lib/useScreenFocused.ts`), so `go`
type-checks. `Button` has no destructive-outline variant: `destructive` plus a hairline border is the outline look the
Elsewhere board shows. `SettingsRow` takes `leading`, `trailing`, `subtitle` and `value` (`components/ui/settings-list.tsx`).
The footer sits under the group, as in the old section.

`recapTheme.ts`:

```ts
import { contrast, hexAlpha, mixHex } from './colour';
import { KIP_COLOURS, type KipColour } from './kip/kipArt';
// …
const INK_GROUND = '#0A0B0E';

/** Every recap image is tinted by your KIP (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §7.7): a dark ground of your colour. */
export function recapTint(colour: KipColour): RecapTint {
  const accent = KIP_COLOURS[colour];
  const ground = mixHex(INK_GROUND, accent, 0.16).toUpperCase();
  const text = mixHex('#FFFFFF', accent, 0.06).toUpperCase();
  return {
    ground,
    accent,
    accentText: readable(accent, ground),
    text,
    soft: mixHex(mixHex('#FFFFFF', accent, 0.18), ground, 0.1).toUpperCase(),
    muted: readable(mixHex(mixHex('#FFFFFF', accent, 0.3), ground, 0.3), ground),
    surface: hexAlpha(text, 0.07),
    border: hexAlpha(text, 0.12),
    hairline: hexAlpha(text, 0.12),
    dot: hexAlpha(accent, 0.17),
    track: hexAlpha(text, 0.35),
  };
}
```

`storyRingColor(colour, scheme)` keeps its loop and starts from `KIP_COLOURS[colour]`. Update the header comment: "tinted
by your KIP" replaces "by its coach". `mixHex` in `lib/colour` returns lower case, so keep the `.toUpperCase()` calls
the old code had.

`badgeArt.ts`:

```ts
import { KIP_COLOURS, kipRamp, type KipColour } from './kip/kipArt';

/** Level V: your KIP's ring, a deep fill and a light glyph (spec §7.7). */
export function kipTier(colour: KipColour): TierColors {
  const ramp = kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS[colour]);
  return { ring: ramp.body, fill: mixHex(ramp.deep, '#000000', 0.5), glyph: ramp.light };
}

export function tierColors(level: number, colour: KipColour): TierColors {
  const l = Math.max(0, Math.min(5, Math.round(level)));
  return l === 5 ? kipTier(colour) : TIERS[l]!;
}
```

`tierTextColor(level, colour, dark)` keeps its body, with `colour` in place of `coachAccent`. `badgeArt`'s own `mixHex`
upper-cases its output; the test compares with `lib/colour`'s. Use `badgeArt`'s `mixHex` in both places, or upper-case
in the test, so the case matches.

```ts
import fs from 'fs';

// The pixel coach cast is gone (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §1): nothing may import it again.

const SCANNED = ['src', '__tests__', 'jest-mocks'].flatMap((d) => sourceFiles(path.join(ROOT, d))).concat(path.join(ROOT, 'App.tsx'));
const CAST = /from\s+['"][^'"]*\/(components\/characters|characters)\/[^'"]*['"]|require\(\s*['"][^'"]*\/(components\/characters|characters)\//;
```
