Create `mobile/__tests__/conventions/kipCopy.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import ts from 'typescript';
import { sourceFiles } from '../../jest-mocks/sourceScan';

// Spec §7.6: user-facing strings say KIP, never "Coach", and none of the 15 retired names survive. Only string
// literals are scanned (the TypeScript scanner skips comments), in src/screens, src/components, src/lib and
// src/navigation. Route names and type names are allowed.
const SRC = path.join(__dirname, '../../src');
const DIRS = ['screens', 'components', 'lib', 'navigation'].map((d) => path.join(SRC, d));
const RETIRED = ['Mochi', 'Boba', 'Sprout', 'Avo', 'Peep', 'Bun', 'Kit', 'Axo', 'Boo', 'Cap', 'Jelly', 'Pengu', 'Luna', 'Gloop', 'Bao'];
const ALLOWED = new Set([
  // Route names (deep links and navigateToCoachEntry keep them; spec §7.1).
  'Coach', 'CoachConsent', 'CoachMemory',
]);

function literals(file: string): string[] {
  const scanner = ts.createScanner(ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true, ts.LanguageVariant.JSX, fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8'));
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (let kind = scanner.scan(); kind !== ts.SyntaxKind.EndOfFileToken; kind = scanner.scan()) {
    if (kind === ts.SyntaxKind.StringLiteral || kind === ts.SyntaxKind.NoSubstitutionTemplateLiteral || kind === ts.SyntaxKind.TemplateHead
      || kind === ts.SyntaxKind.TemplateMiddle || kind === ts.SyntaxKind.TemplateTail || kind === ts.SyntaxKind.JsxText) {
      out.push(scanner.getTokenValue() ?? scanner.getTokenText());
    }
  }
  return out;
}

const FILES = DIRS.flatMap((d) => sourceFiles(d));

it('no user-facing string says "Coach"', () => {
  const hits = FILES.flatMap((f) => literals(f).filter((s) => /\bCoach\b/.test(s) && !ALLOWED.has(s.trim())).map((s) => `${path.relative(SRC, f)}: ${s}`));
  expect(hits).toEqual([]);
});

it('none of the 15 retired coach names appear in any string anywhere in src', () => {
  const all = sourceFiles(SRC);
  const name = new RegExp(`\\b(${RETIRED.join('|')})\\b`);
  const hits = all.flatMap((f) => literals(f).filter((s) => name.test(s)).map((s) => `${path.relative(SRC, f)}: ${s}`));
  expect(hits).toEqual([]);
});
```

`typescript` is already a devDependency (`~6.0.3`). The scanner sees JSX text only when a file is scanned as JSX;
`LanguageVariant.JSX` covers `.tsx`, and `.ts` files have no JSX. Words like "Kit" inside a longer word are not
matched, thanks to `\b`. A legitimate string that trips the guard, such as an icon name or a test ID containing
"Coach", goes in `ALLOWED` with a comment saying why. Route names are the only expected entries.

Create `mobile/__tests__/navigation/noPicker.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { MODAL_ROUTES } from '../../src/navigation/navigationRef';

it('has no picker or thinking-style route, and RecapStory is the only modal (spec §8)', () => {
  expect([...MODAL_ROUTES]).toEqual(['RecapStory']);
  const nav = fs.readFileSync(path.join(__dirname, '../../src/navigation/RootNavigator.tsx'), 'utf8');
  expect(nav).not.toMatch(/MeetYourCoach|ThinkingStyle\b/);
});
```

Create `mobile/__tests__/icons/buildIcons.test.ts`:

```ts
import fs from 'fs';
import path from 'path';
import { ICON_FILES, blockGrid, faceGrid, monochromeGrid, rasterize, spriteGrid, tintedGrid } from '../../src/lib/kip/iconArt';
import { KIP_COLOURS, kipRamp } from '../../src/lib/kip/kipArt';

const MOBILE = path.join(__dirname, '../..');
const app = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(MOBILE, 'app.json'), 'utf8')).expo;

/** Width and height from a PNG's IHDR chunk (bytes 16–23, big-endian). */
function pngSize(file: string): [number, number] {
  const b = fs.readFileSync(path.join(MOBILE, file));
  expect(b.subarray(1, 4).toString('latin1')).toBe('PNG');
  return [b.readUInt32BE(16), b.readUInt32BE(20)];
}
const pixel = (px: Uint8Array, size: number, x: number, y: number) => Array.from(px.subarray((y * size + x) * 4, (y * size + x) * 4 + 4));
const hex = (h: string) => [parseInt(h.slice(1, 3), 16), parseInt(h.slice(3, 5), 16), parseInt(h.slice(5, 7), 16), 255];

describe('the icon art is KIP\'s engine', () => {
  const coral = kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS.coral);

  it('A is the coral block full bleed: body, shade on the right, two eyes, opaque', () => {
    const px = rasterize(faceGrid(), 1024);
    expect(pixel(px, 1024, 0, 0)).toEqual(hex(coral.body));
    expect(pixel(px, 1024, 1023, 0)).toEqual(hex(coral.shade));
    expect(pixel(px, 1024, 320, 440)).toEqual(hex(coral.eyes));
    for (let i = 3; i < px.length; i += 4) if (px[i] !== 255) throw new Error('icon.png must be opaque');
  });

  it('J (dark) sits on #0A0B0E; tinted is greyscale on transparent; monochrome cuts the eyes out', () => {
    expect(pixel(rasterize(blockGrid(), 1024), 1024, 0, 0)).toEqual(hex('#0A0B0E'));
    const tinted = rasterize(tintedGrid(), 1024);
    expect(pixel(tinted, 1024, 0, 0)[3]).toBe(0);
    for (const row of tintedGrid()) for (const c of row) if (c) expect(c.slice(1, 3)).toBe(c.slice(3, 5));
    const mono = monochromeGrid();
    expect([mono[11]![11], mono[15]![15], mono[0]![0]]).toEqual(['#FFFFFF', null, null]);
  });

  it('the splash is the engine sprite at 8 px a cell, centred on transparent', () => {
    const px = rasterize(spriteGrid(), 1024, 8);
    expect(pixel(px, 1024, 0, 0)[3]).toBe(0);
    // Body row 6, column 5 of the sprite → pixel (448 + 40, 448 + 48).
    expect(pixel(px, 1024, 488, 496)).toEqual(hex(coral.body));
  });
});

describe('the files app.json names', () => {
  it.each(ICON_FILES.map((f) => [f.file, f.size] as const))('%s exists at %i × %i', (file, size) => {
    expect(pngSize(file)).toEqual([size, size]);
  });

  it('app.json points at every icon, the iOS light/dark/tinted set and the splash plugin', () => {
    const named = [
      app.icon, app.ios.icon.light, app.ios.icon.dark, app.ios.icon.tinted,
      app.android.adaptiveIcon.foregroundImage, app.android.adaptiveIcon.backgroundImage, app.android.adaptiveIcon.monochromeImage,
      app.web.favicon,
    ];
    const splash = app.plugins.find((p: unknown) => Array.isArray(p) && p[0] === 'expo-splash-screen');
    expect(splash?.[1]).toEqual({ image: './assets/splash-icon.png', imageWidth: 128, resizeMode: 'contain', backgroundColor: '#0A0B0E' });
    for (const f of [...named, splash[1].image]) {
      expect(fs.existsSync(path.join(MOBILE, f))).toBe(true);
      expect(ICON_FILES.map((i) => `./${i.file}`)).toContain(f);
    }
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The icon module**

Create `mobile/src/lib/kip/iconArt.ts`:

```ts
// The app icon (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §9): direction A, KIP's face close-up, plus its platform variants, drawn
// from KIP's engine so the icon can never drift from KIP. Grids and the raster are pure; scripts/build-icons.mjs
// writes the PNGs. Geometry from docs/design/app-icon/project/Icon.dc.html (`C.face`, `C.block`, `C.a_*`, `sprite`).
import { DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR, KIP_COLOURS, kipGrid, kipRamp, type KipColour } from './kipArt';

/** A square grid of cells: '#RRGGBB', 'rgba(r,g,b,a)' or null (transparent). */
export type IconGrid = (string | null)[][];

const DARK = '#0A0B0E';
const CARD = '#14161B';

function grid(n: number, fill: string | null = null): IconGrid {
  return Array.from({ length: n }, () => Array<string | null>(n).fill(fill));
}

function rect(g: IconGrid, x: number, y: number, w: number, h: number, c: string | null): void {
  for (let j = y; j < y + h; j++) for (let i = x; i < x + w; i++) if (g[j] && i >= 0 && i < g.length) g[j]![i] = c;
}

/** A: the tile is KIP's block, full bleed: body, the shade side on the right, two 3 × 3 eyes (24 cells). */
export function faceGrid(colour: KipColour = DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR): IconGrid {
  const r = kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS[colour]);
  const g = grid(24, r.body);
  rect(g, 19, 0, 5, 24, r.shade);
  rect(g, 6, 9, 3, 3, r.eyes);
  rect(g, 14, 9, 3, 3, r.eyes);
  return g;
}

/** J: one block and two eyes on the app's dark ground (the iOS dark icon). The contact shadow is pre-composited. */
export function blockGrid(colour: KipColour = DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR): IconGrid {
  const r = kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS[colour]);
  const g = grid(24, DARK);
  rect(g, 0, 18, 24, 6, CARD);
  rect(g, 6, 6, 9, 12, r.body);
  rect(g, 15, 6, 3, 12, r.shade);
  rect(g, 9, 9, 2, 2, r.eyes);
  rect(g, 13, 9, 2, 2, r.eyes);
  rect(g, 5, 18, 14, 1, '#0D0F13');
  return g;
}

const grey = (hex: string) => {
  const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
  const l = Math.round(0.299 * ((n >> 16) & 255) + 0.587 * ((n >> 8) & 255) + 0.114 * (n & 255));
  const h = l.toString(16).padStart(2, '0');
  return `#${h}${h}${h}`;
};

/** J in greyscale on transparent: iOS applies the tint (spec §9). The ground and the card are dropped. */
export function tintedGrid(colour: KipColour = DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR): IconGrid {
  const g = blockGrid(colour);
  return g.map((row, y) => row.map((c) => (c === null || c === DARK || c === CARD || y >= 18 ? null : grey(c))));
}

/** Android adaptive background, 108 dp at 3 dp a cell (36 cells): the body and the shade side. */
export function adaptiveBackgroundGrid(colour: KipColour = DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR): IconGrid {
  const r = kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS[colour]);
  const g = grid(36, r.body);
  rect(g, 25, 0, 11, 36, r.shade);
  return g;
}

/** Android adaptive foreground: the eyes, inside the 66 dp safe circle, on transparent. */
export function adaptiveForegroundGrid(colour: KipColour = DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR): IconGrid {
  const r = kipRamp(KIP_COLOURS[colour]);
  const g = grid(36);
  rect(g, 12, 15, 3, 3, r.eyes);
  rect(g, 20, 15, 3, 3, r.eyes);
  return g;
}

/** Android monochrome: the J block in white with the eyes cut out. */
export function monochromeGrid(): IconGrid {
  const g = grid(36);
  rect(g, 11, 11, 14, 14, '#FFFFFF');
  rect(g, 14, 14, 3, 3, null);
  rect(g, 19, 14, 3, 3, null);
  return g;
}

/** The engine's KIP sprite (rest, idle) on transparent: the splash. */
export function spriteGrid(colour: KipColour = DEFAULT_KIP_COLOUR): IconGrid {
  return kipGrid({ colour, pose: 'rest', mood: 'idle' }).map((row) => [...row]);
}

function rgba(c: string | null): [number, number, number, number] {
  if (!c) return [0, 0, 0, 0];
  if (c.startsWith('#')) {
    const n = parseInt(c.slice(1), 16);
    return [(n >> 16) & 255, (n >> 8) & 255, n & 255, 255];
  }
  const m = c.match(/[\d.]+/g)!.map(Number);
  return [m[0]!, m[1]!, m[2]!, Math.round((m[3] ?? 1) * 255)];
}

/**
 * The grid as RGBA pixels, size × size, nearest neighbour: pixel (x, y) takes cell floor(x · n / size). With
 * `scale` (pixels per cell, the splash) the grid is drawn at that size, centred on a transparent canvas.
 */
export function rasterize(g: IconGrid, size: number, scale?: number): Uint8Array {
  const n = g.length;
  const out = new Uint8Array(size * size * 4);
  const cell = scale ?? size / n;
  const offset = scale ? Math.floor((size - n * scale) / 2) : 0;
  for (let y = 0; y < size; y++) for (let x = 0; x < size; x++) {
    const cx = Math.floor((x - offset) / cell), cy = Math.floor((y - offset) / cell);
    const c = cx >= 0 && cy >= 0 && cx < n && cy < n ? g[cy]![cx]! : null;
    out.set(rgba(c), (y * size + x) * 4);
  }
  return out;
}

export interface IconFile {
  file: string;
  size: number;
  grid: () => IconGrid;
  /** Pixels per cell when the art is centred instead of filling the square (the splash). */
  scale?: number;
}

/** Every file app.json names (spec §9 table). */
export const ICON_FILES: readonly IconFile[] = [
  { file: 'assets/icon.png', size: 1024, grid: () => faceGrid() },
  { file: 'assets/icon-dark.png', size: 1024, grid: () => blockGrid() },
  { file: 'assets/icon-tinted.png', size: 1024, grid: () => tintedGrid() },
  { file: 'assets/android-icon-background.png', size: 432, grid: () => adaptiveBackgroundGrid() },
  { file: 'assets/android-icon-foreground.png', size: 432, grid: () => adaptiveForegroundGrid() },
  { file: 'assets/android-icon-monochrome.png', size: 432, grid: () => monochromeGrid() },
  { file: 'assets/splash-icon.png', size: 1024, grid: () => spriteGrid(), scale: 8 },
  { file: 'assets/favicon.png', size: 48, grid: () => faceGrid() },
];
```

- [ ] **Step 4: The build script and the dependencies**

From `mobile/`, run:

```bash
npm install --save-dev --save-exact pngjs@7.0.0
npx expo install expo-splash-screen
```

Create `mobile/scripts/build-icons.mjs`:

```js
// Writes the app icons from KIP's engine (spec 2026-10-14 KIP Coach §9): `npm run build:icons`, which runs
// `sucrase-node scripts/build-icons.mjs`. sucrase-node's require hook compiles the TypeScript grid module, so the icon
// can never drift from KIP. Run it again after any change to lib/kip/kipArt.ts or lib/kip/iconArt.ts.
import { createRequire } from 'node:module';
import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const require = createRequire(import.meta.url);
const { PNG } = require('pngjs');
const { ICON_FILES, rasterize } = require('../src/lib/kip/iconArt.ts');

const root = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
for (const { file, size, grid, scale } of ICON_FILES) {
  const png = new PNG({ width: size, height: size });
  png.data = Buffer.from(rasterize(grid(), size, scale));
  const out = join(root, file);
  mkdirSync(dirname(out), { recursive: true });
  writeFileSync(out, PNG.sync.write(png, { colorType: 6 }));
  console.log(`${file} ${size}×${size}`);
}
```

In `package.json` `scripts`, add `"build:icons": "sucrase-node scripts/build-icons.mjs"`.

Run `npm run build:icons`. Expected output: eight lines, `assets/icon.png 1024×1024` through `assets/favicon.png 48×48`.

Open `assets/icon.png`, `icon-dark.png` and `splash-icon.png` in Preview (`open assets/icon.png`) and compare them with
`docs/design/app-icon/project/Main.dc.html` A and `Variants.dc.html`.

- [ ] **Step 5: `app.json`**

In `mobile/app.json`, `expo.ios` gains:

```json
      "icon": {
        "light": "./assets/icon.png",
        "dark": "./assets/icon-dark.png",
        "tinted": "./assets/icon-tinted.png"
      },
```

`expo.android.adaptiveIcon` keeps its four keys and file names; the files are new. `expo.plugins` gains, after
`"expo-asset"`:

```json
      [
        "expo-splash-screen",
        {
          "image": "./assets/splash-icon.png",
          "imageWidth": 128,
          "resizeMode": "contain",
          "backgroundColor": "#0A0B0E"
        }
      ],
```

`npx expo install` may already have added a bare `"expo-splash-screen"` string to `plugins`. If so, replace it with the
array above so there is exactly one entry. `app.config.js` copies `plugins`, so it needs no change. The icon ships in a
store build, not an OTA update.

## Self-review (2026-10-14, against the spec)

**Spec coverage, section by section:**

| Spec | Task |
| --- | --- |
| §0 owner decisions: cast removed, KIP is the coach, Ask KIP, W7, poses, weather window, every thread capability kept, v2 art quality, no answer style, icon A, slot-driven engine | 1–15 (no answer-style row: Task 12's test asserts its absence) |
| §0 defaults: no consent bump, level V "Legend", offline from network errors | 4, 13, 2 |
| §1 the cast table, conventions entries for deleted files | 9 (picker branch), 12 (rows), 14 (everything else) |
| §1 decoupling 1–5 (thinking-text ids, KIP lines, colour and clock, `CoachStatusProvider`, device storage) | 1, 2 |
| §2 persona v5, `resolvePersona` mapping, prompts, no persona guards, telemetry, eval | 3, 4, 0 / 16 |
| §2 migration, routes for older apps, `Person.coachId` (resolves to `kip` with no change), consent wording | 4 |
| §3.1–3.4 the header, the room card, the hot tag extraction, the diorama, the bubble, the label, the status line, every state, offline, "Not now" | 9 |
| §3.5 collapse, the thumbnail, "Shrink room" persistence; §3.6 Reduce Motion | 10 (loops off: 8) |
| §4 poses, the turn, facing, the gallery, 4 points per KIP pixel | 5, 6 (`drawKipLayer`), 10 (turn) |
| §5 modules, the starter furnishing, loops, the performance budget | 6, 8 (device timing: 16) |
| §6 `useTodayScene`, the window states, recompute rules, failed-read fallback | 7, 6 (`roomWeatherOf`) |
| §7.1 tab label and hub | 11 |
| §7.2 Ask KIP bars, `KipHead` | 11 (`KipHead`: 1) |
| §7.3 Home's Ask KIP and KIP's note | 11 |
| §7.3 recap renderers and tint | 13 |
| §7.4 Conversations and memory | 12 |
| §7.5 the Settings KIP group, the hosted-engine rename | 12 |
| §7.6 navigator titles and copy | 12 |
| §7.6 the copy guard | 14 |
| §7.7 every swap and the recap and badge data paths | 13 |
| §8 routes removed, `MODAL_ROUTES`, first open | 9 (no picker), 14 |
| §9 the icon set, `build-icons.mjs` via sucrase-node, `pngjs`, `app.json`, the splash plugin | 15 |
| §10 rules | Global Constraints |
| §11 privacy | Global Constraints; nothing new is sent (no prompt change beyond the persona) |
| §12 tests | each task's Step 1; the guards in 14 |
| §13 out of scope | not built |

**Where the spec cannot be followed literally, and the ruling taken:**
1. **§3.2's crop numbers do not come from one formula.** "375-point phone → 323-point inner width" means 52 points
   are taken off; "320-point phone → 280" means 40. The plan does not hard-code either: `RoomCard` measures the frame's
   inner width (`onLayout`) and `roomCrop` centres the focus box in whatever it gets. Tests cover 280, 323, 335 and
   375. At 375 (wider than the 340-point room) the room is centred, never scaled.
2. **"{coach}'s note for the week" (§7.3) does not exist.** Ruling 9 renames the three strings that do.
3. **The "Settings" group (§7.5) lives in Profile sections** (`ProfileSectionScreen`, `profileHub`). See ruling 10.
4. **There is no v2 starter set** to port (§5 cites `k4room.js`, which is v1). Ruling 2: the starter room is authored
   on the v2 grid with the v2 helpers, and its layout makes §4's "mirrored, glancing toward the desk" literally true.
5. **ThinkingTextScreen's preview, "type / glance" (§7.7),** is the thinking row, which shows KIP's head (`glance`).
   The typing arms are below the head's rows, so the preview shows the glance only.
6. **The monitor's content** follows the stage (§3.4). It is kept out of the static picture (ruling 5), so §5's "a pose
   change never rebuilds the picture" holds for state changes too.

**Placeholder scan:** no "TBD" / "TODO" / "similar to". Where a step depends on a detail this plan could not read
(a fixture helper's exact name in an existing test file), the step names the file and line to copy from, and the
expected behaviour.

**Type consistency:** these names are used identically across tasks:
- `KipStage`, `kipStage`, `kipStatusLine`, `roomLabel`, `hotTagLabel`;
- `RoomView` props, `RoomLoadout`, `KipWorn`, `drawRoom`, `drawRoomStatic`, `drawRoomFrames`, `drawKipLayer`,
  `roomPictureKey`, `kipLayerKey`, `FOCUS_BOX`, `THUMB_BOX`, `MONITOR_GLASS`, `roomWeatherOf`;
- `useTodayScene().window`, `.recovery`, `.scene`;
- `CoachStatusValue.offline` / `setOffline`, `markNetworkError` / `isNetworkError`;
- `useKipTurn`, `collapseProgress`, `readKipRoomShrunk` / `writeKipRoomShrunk`;
- `recapTint(colour)`, `tierColors(level, colour)`, `kipTier`;
- `ICON_FILES`, `rasterize`.

**Review Focus:** each of the five lines has its pinning test in the owning task (Tasks 2, 4, 8, 9 and 10). The
caption-row wrap is checked by eye in Task 16.
